## 1. Greetings and Polite Expressions (Salutations et politesse)

| French (with Article) | English Meaning | Pronunciation | Audio | Gender / Word Type | Example Sentence | Plural / Conjugation | Liaison & Teacher's Notes |
|:---|:---|:---|:---:|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| **coucou** | Hey there / Peek-a-boo (affectionate informal greeting) | `koo-KOO` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=coucou&op=translate) | `Interjection (informal)` | Coucou tout le monde, je suis enfin là ! *(Hey everyone, I'm finally here!)* | Invariable interjection | Used among family, close friends, or text messages [kuku]. |
| **à la prochaine** | Until next time / See you next time | `ah lah proh-SHEN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=%C3%A0%20la%20prochaine&op=translate) | `Farewell Phrase` | Merci pour cet échange enrichissant, à la prochaine ! *(Thank you for this enriching conversation, until next time!)* | Invariable farewell | Short for 'à la prochaine fois' [a la pʁɔʃɛn]. |
| **à plus** | See ya / Later (casual farewell) | `ah PLOOSS` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=%C3%A0%20plus&op=translate) | `Colloquial Farewell` | Je file en cours, à plus ! *(I'm off to class, see ya!)* | Invariable phrase | Final 's' IS sounded in this greeting: [a plys]! Often texted as 'A+'. |
| **avec plaisir** | With pleasure / My pleasure | `ah-vek pleh-ZEER` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=avec%20plaisir&op=translate) | `Polite Response` | — Tu viens prendre un thé ? — Avec grand plaisir ! *(— Are you coming to have tea? — With great pleasure!)* | Invariable phrase | Sounded 'c' and 'r' [avɛk pleziʁ]. Warm alternative to 'de rien'. |
| **je vous remercie** | I thank you (formal) | `zhuh voo ruh-mair-SEE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=je%20vous%20remercie&op=translate) | `Polite Formula` | Je vous remercie chaleureusement pour votre aide précieuse. *(I thank you warmly for your valuable help.)* | Informal: je te remercie | More formal and heartfelt than a simple 'merci'. Regular -er verb remercier. |
| **mille mercis** | A thousand thanks / Thanks a million | `meel mair-SEE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=mille%20mercis&op=translate) | `Expression of Gratitude` | Mille mercis pour votre généreuse invitation ! *(A thousand thanks for your generous invitation!)* | mille is invariable (never adds -s) | 'Mille' (thousand) never takes an 's' in French. Double 'll' sounds [l] [mil]. |
| **il n'y a pas de quoi** | Don't mention it / You're welcome | `eel nyah pah duh KWAH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=il%20n%27y%20a%20pas%20de%20quoi&op=translate) | `Polite Locution` | — Merci d'avoir porté mon sac. — Il n'y a pas de quoi ! *(— Thanks for carrying my bag. — Don't mention it!)* | Often contracted to: Y'a pas de quoi | Literally 'there is not of what' = no reason to thank me [il nja pa də kwa]. |
| **pas de souci** | No worries / No problem | `pah duh soo-SEE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=pas%20de%20souci&op=translate) | `Everyday Locution` | Ne t'en fais pas pour le retard, pas de souci ! *(Don't worry about being late, no worries!)* | Invariable everyday phrase | Extremely frequent modern spoken phrase [pa də susi]. Noun: le souci (worry). |
| **pas de problème** | No problem | `pah duh proh-BLEM` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=pas%20de%20probl%C3%A8me&op=translate) | `Everyday Locution` | Je peux t'emprunter ton stylo ? — Oui, pas de problème ! *(Can I borrow your pen? — Yes, no problem!)* | Invariable phrase | Gender reminder: 'le problème' is MASCULINE (Greek origin in -ème). |
| **c'est gentil** | That's kind / That's sweet of you | `seh zhahn-TEE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=c%27est%20gentil&op=translate) | `Appreciation Phrase` | Merci pour les fleurs, c'est vraiment très gentil de ta part. *(Thanks for the flowers, that's really very kind of you.)* | Invariable expression | Silent 'l' in gentil [ʒɑ̃ti]. Common way to accept a favor or gift. |
| **navré / navrée** | Deeply sorry / Truly apologetic | `nah-VRAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=navr%C3%A9%20/%20navr%C3%A9e&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Je suis navré pour ce malentendu regrettable. *(I am deeply sorry for this regrettable misunderstanding.)* | navrés / navrées | More formal and sincere than pardon: Je suis navré(e) [navʁe]. |
| **toutes mes excuses** | My apologies / I apologize sincerely | `toot may zek-SKYOOS` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=toutes%20mes%20excuses&op=translate) | `Apology Locution` | Toutes mes excuses pour cet appel tardif. *(All my apologies for this late call.)* | une excuse -> des excuses | Mandatory [z] liaison: mes‿excuses [me-z-ɛkskyz]. Feminine plural noun. |
| **ce n'est pas grave** | It doesn't matter / No big deal | `suh neh pah GRAHV` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=ce%20n%27est%20pas%20grave&op=translate) | `Reassurance Phrase` | Tu as oublié le livre ? Ce n'est pas grave, apporte-le demain. *(You forgot the book? No big deal, bring it tomorrow.)* | Colloquial: C'est pas grave | In everyday spoken French, the 'ne' is almost always omitted: [se pa ɡʁav]. |
| **bon après-midi** | Have a good afternoon | `bohn nah-preh-mee-DEE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=bon%20apr%C3%A8s-midi&op=translate) | `Farewell Wish (m.)` | Au revoir et bon après-midi à vous ! *(Goodbye and have a good afternoon to you!)* | les bons après-midis | Mandatory [n] liaison: bon‿après-midi [bɔ̃.n‿apʁɛmidi]. Said when leaving between 12:00 and 17:00. |
| **bon week-end** | Have a nice weekend | `bohn week-END` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=bon%20week-end&op=translate) | `Farewell Wish (m.)` | Bonne fin de semaine et bon week-end à tous ! *(Have a good end of the week and a nice weekend everyone!)* | les bons week-ends | Borrowed from English, masculine in French [wikɛnd]. Said on Friday afternoons. |
| **bonnes vacances** | Have a great vacation / holiday | `buhn vah-KAHNSS` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=bonnes%20vacances&op=translate) | `Greeting Wish (f. pl.)` | C'est le dernier jour de travail, bonnes vacances ! *(It's the last day of work, have a great holiday!)* | Always feminine plural! | Adjective bonne agrees in feminine plural: bonnes vacances [bɔn vakɑ̃s]. |
| **bon retour** | Safe trip back / Safe journey home | `bohn ruh-TOOR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=bon%20retour&op=translate) | `Farewell Wish (m.)` | Merci pour votre visite et bon retour chez vous ! *(Thank you for your visit and safe trip back home!)* | les bons retours | Masculine noun [ʁətuʁ]. Wish said to visitors departing for home. |
| **bonne continuation** | Best wishes for the future / Keep up the good work | `buhn kohn-tee-nyoo-ah-SYOHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=bonne%20continuation&op=translate) | `Farewell Locution (f.)` | Au revoir et bonne continuation dans vos nouveaux projets ! *(Goodbye and all the best with your new projects!)* | les bonnes continuations | Nouns in -tion are feminine [kɔ̃tinɥasjɔ̃]. Popular French farewell wishing ongoing success. |
| **au plaisir** | Looking forward to seeing you / Pleasure meeting you | `oh pleh-ZEER` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=au%20plaisir&op=translate) | `Polite Farewell` | C'était un réel bonheur d'échanger avec vous, au plaisir ! *(It was a real joy speaking with you, until next time!)* | Short for: Au plaisir de vous revoir | Elegant closing formula in spoken French and emails [o pleziʁ]. |
| **à tout de suite** | See you in a moment / Right away | `ah toot SWEET` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=%C3%A0%20tout%20de%20suite&op=translate) | `Farewell Phrase` | Je descends dans le hall d'entrée, à tout de suite ! *(I'm coming down to the entrance lobby, see you in a second!)* | Invariable phrase | Distinction: 'à tout à l'heure' = later today; 'à tout de suite' = within 1-5 minutes! |
| **à un de ces quatre** | See you around / Catch you one of these days | `ah œ̃ duh say KAHT-ruh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=%C3%A0%20un%20de%20ces%20quatre&op=translate) | `Casual Idiom` | Prends soin de toi vieux, à un de ces quatre ! *(Take care buddy, see you around!)* | Short for: à un de ces quatre matins | Vivid French idiom literally meaning 'see you on one of these four mornings' [a œ̃ də se katʁ]. |
| **ravi / ravie** | Delighted / Thrilled to meet you | `rah-VEE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=ravi%20/%20ravie&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Ravi de faire votre connaissance, monsieur Laurent. *(Delighted to meet you, Mr. Laurent.)* | ravis / ravies | Elegant synonym of 'enchanté(e)'. Feminine adds silent '-e'. [ʁavi]. |
| **quel plaisir de vous voir** | What a pleasure to see you | `kel pleh-ZEER duh voo VWAHR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=quel%20plaisir%20de%20vous%20voir&op=translate) | `Warm Welcome Greeting` | Entrez donc, quel plaisir de vous voir enfin ! *(Do come in, what a pleasure to finally see you!)* | Informal: quel plaisir de te voir | Liaison: quel‿plaisir no liaison [kɛl pleziʁ]. |
| **quoi de neuf ?** | What's new? / What's up? | `KWAH duh NUF` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=quoi%20de%20neuf%20%3F&op=translate) | `Casual Question` | Salut Marc, ça fait longtemps ! Quoi de neuf depuis l'été ? *(Hi Marc, long time no see! What's new since summer?)* | Invariable casual question | Sounded 'f' [kwa də nœf]. Common response: 'Pas grand-chose' (Not much). |
| **comment ça va ?** | How are things going? / How are you? | `koh-mahn sah VAH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=comment%20%C3%A7a%20va%20%3F&op=translate) | `Conversational Inquiry` | Bonjour Claire, comment ça va aujourd'hui ? *(Hello Claire, how are things going today?)* | Invariable question | More polite and complete than just 'Ça va ?' [kɔmɑ̃ sa va]. |
| **ça roule ?** | How's it rolling? / Everything good? | `sah ROOL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=%C3%A7a%20roule%20%3F&op=translate) | `Slang / Familiar Question` | Salut les amis, ça roule pour vous ce matin ? *(Hi guys, everything rolling fine for you this morning?)* | Invariable youth slang | From the verb rouler (to roll) [sa ʁul]. Highly popular among young native speakers. |
| **comme ci, comme ça** | So-so / Neither good nor bad | `kohm SEE kohm SAH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=comme%20ci%2C%20comme%20%C3%A7a&op=translate) | `Idiomatic Response` | — Tu as passé une bonne journée ? — Bof, comme ci, comme ça. *(— Did you have a good day? — Meh, so-so.)* | Invariable phrase | Cedilla ç ensures [s] sound before 'a' [kɔm si kɔm sa]. |
| **bon rétablissement** | Get well soon / Speedy recovery | `bohn ray-tah-blees-MAHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=bon%20r%C3%A9tablissement&op=translate) | `Wishing Phrase (m.)` | J'ai appris que tu avais la grippe, bon rétablissement ! *(I heard you had the flu, get well soon!)* | les bons rétablissements | Nouns in -ment are masculine [ʁetablismɑ̃]. Standard wish to an ill person. |
| **joyeux anniversaire** | Happy birthday | `zhwah-YUH zah-nee-vair-SAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=joyeux%20anniversaire&op=translate) | `Celebration Wish (m.)` | Tous mes vœux pour tes vingt ans, joyeux anniversaire ! *(Best wishes for your 20th birthday, happy birthday!)* | les joyeux anniversaires | Mandatory [z] liaison: joyeux‿anniversaire [ʒwajø-z-anivɛʁsɛʁ]! Also: 'Bon anniversaire !'. |
| **meilleurs vœux** | Best wishes / Season's greetings | `meh-YUHR VUH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=meilleurs%20v%C5%93ux&op=translate) | `Greeting Locution (m. pl.)` | Bonne année et meilleurs vœux de santé et de bonheur ! *(Happy New Year and best wishes of health and happiness!)* | un vœu -> les vœux (takes -x) | Ligature œ in vœu [vø]. Traditional New Year greeting in France during January. |

## 2. Self-Introduction and Identity (Présentation et identité)

| French (with Article) | English Meaning | Pronunciation | Audio | Gender / Word Type | Example Sentence | Plural / Conjugation | Liaison & Teacher's Notes |
|:---|:---|:---|:---:|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| **la présentation** | Introduction / Presentation | `lah pray-zahn-tah-SYOHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20pr%C3%A9sentation&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Faisons un tour de table pour les présentations. *(Let's go around the table for introductions.)* | les présentations | Nouns in -tion are feminine [pʁezɑ̃tasjɔ̃]. |
| **l'identité (f.)** | Identity | `lee-dahn-tee-TAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27identit%C3%A9%20%28f.%29&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Veuillez présenter une pièce d'identité avec photo. *(Please present a photo ID.)* | les identités | Nouns ending in -ité are feminine [idɑ̃tite]. |
| **la citoyenneté** | Citizenship | `lah see-twah-yahn-TAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20citoyennet%C3%A9&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Il a obtenu la citoyenneté française par naturalisation. *(He obtained French citizenship by naturalization.)* | les citoyennetés | Nouns in -té are feminine [sitwajɑ̃te]. Adjective: citoyen / citoyenne. |
| **le lieu de résidence** | Place of residence / Legal address | `luh LYUH duh ray-zee-DAHNSS` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20lieu%20de%20r%C3%A9sidence&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Mon lieu de résidence principal se situe dans le centre de Lyon. *(My primary place of residence is located in downtown Lyon.)* | les lieux de résidence (takes -x) | Words in -eu take -x in plural: un lieu -> des lieux [ljø]. |
| **l'état civil (m.)** | Marital status / Civil status | `lay-tah see-VEEL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27%C3%A9tat%20civil%20%28m.%29&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | L'état civil indique si la personne est mariée ou célibataire. *(Civil status indicates whether the person is married or single.)* | les états civils | Silent 't' in état [eta]. Sounded 'l' in civil [sivil]. |
| **pacsé / pacsée** | In a civil partnership / PACSed | `pahk-SAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=pacs%C3%A9%20/%20pacs%C3%A9e&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Ils sont pacsés depuis trois ans à la mairie. *(They have been in a civil partnership for three years at city hall.)* | pacsés / pacsées | Derived from PACS (Pacte Civil de Solidarité), a common French legal partnership [pakse]. |
| **séparé / séparée** | Separated (marital status) | `say-pah-RAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=s%C3%A9par%C3%A9%20/%20s%C3%A9par%C3%A9e&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Ils sont séparés de corps et de biens en attendant le jugement. *(They are legally separated awaiting the court ruling.)* | séparés / séparées | Pronounced identically m./f. [sepaʁe]. Legal status before final divorce. |
| **divorcé / divorcée** | Divorced | `dee-vor-SAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=divorc%C3%A9%20/%20divorc%C3%A9e&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Après cinq ans de vie commune, ils sont divorcés. *(After five years of living together, they are divorced.)* | divorcés / divorcées | Cedilla ç ensures [s] sound before 'e' [divɔʁse]. |
| **veuf / veuve** | Widowed / Widower / Widow | `VUF / VUH-vuh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=veuf%20/%20veuve&op=translate) | `Adjective / Noun (m./f.)` | Cette dame âgée est veuve depuis deux ans. *(This elderly lady has been a widow for two years.)* | veufs / veuves | Pattern -f -> -ve: veuf [vœf] -> veuve [vœv]. |
| **l'adresse (f.)** | Home address | `lah-DRESS` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27adresse%20%28f.%29&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Mon adresse postale est 15, avenue des Fleurs, à Nantes. *(My postal address is 15 Avenue des Fleurs, in Nantes.)* | les adresses | Doubles 'ss' [adʁɛs]. Note: address in French starts with number, then comma, then street! |
| **le code postal** | Postal code / ZIP code | `luh KOHD pohs-TAHL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20code%20postal&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Le code postal de Nice est 06000. *(The postal code of Nice is 06000.)* | les codes postaux (irregular -aux) | Adjective postal -> postaux takes -aux in plural. In France, postal codes always have 5 digits. |
| **les coordonnées (f. pl.)** | Contact details / Contact info | `lay koh-or-doh-NAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=les%20coordonn%C3%A9es%20%28f.%20pl.%29&op=translate) | `Feminine Plural (f. pl.)` | Laissez vos coordonnées sur le répondeur pour être rappelé. *(Leave your contact details on voicemail to be called back.)* | une coordonnée -> des coordonnées | Always plural for contact details [kɔɔʁdɔne]. Covers phone, email, and address. |
| **la boîte aux lettres** | Letterbox / Mailbox | `lah BWAHT oh LET-ruh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20bo%C3%AEte%20aux%20lettres&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Le facteur glisse le courrier dans la boîte aux lettres. *(The mailman drops the letters into the mailbox.)* | les boîtes aux lettres | Circumflex on 'î' [bwat o lɛtʁ]. Contraction aux = à + les. |
| **le sexe** | Sex / Biological gender (on official forms) | `luh SEKSS` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20sexe&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Cochez la case correspondant à votre sexe : M ou F. *(Check the box corresponding to your sex: M or F.)* | les sexes | On French administrative forms: Masculin (M) / Féminin (F) [sɛks]. |
| **le paraphe** | Initials / Paraph (on contract) | `luh pah-RAHF` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20paraphe&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Apposez un paraphe sur chaque page du contrat de vente. *(Put your initials on each page of the sales agreement.)* | les paraphes | 'ph' sounds [f] [paʁaf]. French legal requirement: paraphe on each page, full signature at the end. |
| **le passeport biométrique** | Biometric passport | `luh pahs-POR byoh-may-TREEK` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20passeport%20biom%C3%A9trique&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Le passeport biométrique contient une puce électronique. *(The biometric passport contains an electronic chip.)* | les passeports biométriques | Silent 't' in passeport [paspɔʁ]. |

In France, driving licenses start with 12 points. |

Single 's' pronounced [z]. |
| **faire la connaissance de** | To make the acquaintance of / To meet | `FAIR lah koh-neh-SAHNSS duh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=faire%20la%20connaissance%20de&op=translate) | `Idiomatic Expression` | Je suis très heureux de faire votre connaissance. *(I am very pleased to make your acquaintance.)* | je fais la connaissance, nous faisons la connaissance | Circumflex on 'î' in connaître, but lost in modern spelling of connaissance [kɔnɛsɑ̃s]. |
| **épeler** | To spell out (letters) | `ay-puh-LAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=%C3%A9peler&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group stem-changing)` | Pouvez-vous épeler votre nom de famille, s'il vous plaît ? *(Could you spell your family name, please?)* | j'épelle, tu épelles, il épelle, nous épelons, vous épelez, ils épellent | Stem changer: doubles 'l' before silent endings (j'épelle [epɛl], but nous épelons [eplɔ̃]). |
| **remplir un formulaire** | To fill in a form | `rahn-PLEER œ̃ for-myoo-LAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=remplir%20un%20formulaire&op=translate) | `Expression / Verb remplir` | Veuillez remplir ce formulaire d'inscription en lettres majuscules. *(Please fill in this registration form in capital letters.)* | je remplis, tu remplis, il remplit, nous remplissons, vous remplissez, ils remplissent | 2nd group regular verb in -ir with -iss- infix: nous remplissons [ʁɑ̃plisɔ̃]. |
| **signer** | To sign (a document) | `see-NYAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=signer&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group -er)` | Signez ici avec la mention manuscrite « lu et approuvé ». *(Sign here with the handwritten phrase 'read and approved'.)* | je signe, tu signes, il signe, nous signons, vous signez, ils signent | Palatal gn [ɲ] [siɲe]. Traditional French legal phrase: 'Lu et approuvé'. |
| **résider** | To reside / To live formally | `ray-zee-DAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=r%C3%A9sider&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group -er)` | Mon oncle réside dans un charmant village provençal. *(My uncle resides in a charming Provencal village.)* | je réside, tu résides, il réside, nous résidons, vous résidez, ils résident | Formal synonym of habiter [ʁezide]. Noun: la résidence. |
| **le statut** | Status (employment / legal status) | `luh stah-TOO` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20statut&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Quel est votre statut professionnel actuel ? *(What is your current employment status?)* | les statuts | Silent 't' [staty]. Do not confuse with 'la statue' (monument). |
| **la carte de séjour** | Residence permit / Green card equivalent | `lah KART duh say-ZHOOR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20carte%20de%20s%C3%A9jour&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Il a renouvelé sa carte de séjour pour dix ans. *(He renewed his residence permit for 10 years.)* | les cartes de séjour | Essential administrative document for foreign residents in France. |
| **le titre de séjour** | Residence document / Legal permit | `luh TEET-ruh duh say-ZHOOR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20titre%20de%20s%C3%A9jour&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | La préfecture délivre les titres de séjour aux étrangers. *(The prefecture issues residence documents to foreigners.)* | les titres de séjour | Masculine noun [titʁ də seʒuʁ]. |
| **le domicile** | Legal residence / Domicile / Home | `luh doh-mee-SEEL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20domicile&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Fournissez un justificatif de domicile de moins de trois mois. *(Provide proof of address less than three months old.)* | les domiciles | 'Justificatif de domicile' (electricity/utility bill) is required everywhere in French administration! |
| **avoir ... ans** | To be ... years old | `ah-VWAHR ... AHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=avoir%20...%20ans&op=translate) | `Idiomatic Age Structure` | Mon frère a vingt-cinq ans et ma sœur en a trente. *(My brother is 25 years old and my sister is 30.)* | j'ai 20 ans, tu as 20 ans, il a 20 ans, nous avons 20 ans | Mandatory [z] liaison: vingt‿ans [vɛ̃-t-ɑ̃], deux‿ans [dø-z-ɑ̃]. Remember: NEVER use être for age! |

Followed by preposition 'de'. |

|:---|:---|:---|:---:|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| **le chiffre** | Digit / Figure (0 to 9) | `luh SHEEF-ruh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20chiffre&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Ce code secret comporte quatre chiffres différents. *(This secret code contains four different digits.)* | les chiffres | Distinction: 'chiffre' = digit (0-9); 'nombre' = number/quantity. Chiffre d'affaires = business turnover. |
| **le nombre** | Number / Amount / Quantity | `luh NOHN-bruh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20nombre&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Le nombre de participants a doublé cette année. *(The number of participants doubled this year.)* | les nombres | Nasal vowel [nɔ̃bʁ]. Nombre pair = even number; nombre impair = odd number. |
| **le premier / la première** | First (1st) | `luh pruh-MYAY / lah pruh-MYAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20premier%20/%20la%20premi%C3%A8re&op=translate) | `Ordinal Adjective (m./f.)` | C'est la première fois que je visite la capitale. *(It's the first time that I visit the capital.)* | premiers / premières | Masculine silent 'r' [pʁəmje]; feminine pronounced [pʁəmjɛʁ]. BANGS adjective. |
| **le deuxième / la deuxième** | Second (2nd in a series) | `luh duh-ZYEM / lah duh-ZYEM` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20deuxi%C3%A8me%20/%20la%20deuxi%C3%A8me&op=translate) | `Ordinal Adjective (m./f.)` | Mon bureau se trouve au deuxième étage avec ascenseur. *(My office is on the second floor with elevator.)* | deuxièmes | 'x' pronounced [z] [døzjɛm]. In French, ordinal suffix is -ième (deuxième, troisième, quatrième). |
| **le second / la seconde** | Second (when only two exist) | `luh suh-GOHN / lah suh-GOHND` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20second%20/%20la%20seconde&op=translate) | `Ordinal Adjective (m./f.)` | La Seconde Guerre mondiale s'est terminée en 1945. *(The Second World War ended in 1945.)* | seconds / secondes | Pronunciation trap: 'c' is pronounced [ɡ] as in 'go': [səɡɔ̃] / [səɡɔ̃d]! |
| **le troisième / la troisième** | Third (3rd) | `luh trwah-ZYEM / lah trwah-ZYEM` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20troisi%C3%A8me%20/%20la%20troisi%C3%A8me&op=translate) | `Ordinal Adjective (m./f.)` | Prenez la troisième sortie au rond-point. *(Take the third exit at the roundabout.)* | troisièmes | Single 's' between vowels pronounced [z] [tʁwazjɛm]. |
| **le quatrième** | Fourth (4th) | `luh kah-tree-YEM` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20quatri%C3%A8me&op=translate) | `Ordinal Adjective (m./f.)` | C'est la quatrième porte à gauche dans le couloir. *(It's the fourth door on the left in the hallway.)* | quatrièmes | Final 'e' of quatre dropped before adding -ième: [katʁijɛm]. |
| **le cinquième** | Fifth (5th) | `luh sa(n)-KYEM` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20cinqui%C3%A8me&op=translate) | `Ordinal Adjective (m./f.)` | L'appartement est situé au cinquième et dernier étage. *(The apartment is located on the fifth and top floor.)* | cinquièmes | Spelling rule: adds 'u' after cinq before -ième: cinquième [sɛ̃kjɛm]. |
| **le neuvième** | Ninth (9th) | `luh nuh-VYEM` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20neuvi%C3%A8me&op=translate) | `Ordinal Adjective (m./f.)` | Le neuvième arrondissement de Paris est réputé pour ses théâtres. *(The 9th arrondissement of Paris is famous for its theaters.)* | neuvièmes | Spelling rule: 'f' of neuf changes to 'v' before -ième: neuvième [nøvjɛm]! |
| **le dixième** | Tenth (10th) | `luh deez-YEM` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20dixi%C3%A8me&op=translate) | `Ordinal Adjective (m./f.)` | Ce coureur a terminé à la dixième place du classement. *(This runner finished in 10th place in the rankings.)* | dixièmes | 'x' pronounced [z] [dizjɛm]. |
| **le vingtième** | Twentieth (20th) | `luh va(n)-TYEM` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20vingti%C3%A8me&op=translate) | `Ordinal Adjective (m./f.)` | Le vingtième siècle a connu des bouleversements majeurs. *(The 20th century witnessed major upheavals.)* | vingtièmes | Sounded 't' before -ième: [vɛ̃tjɛm]. |
| **le centième** | Hundredth (100th) | `luh sahn-TYEM` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20centi%C3%A8me&op=translate) | `Ordinal Adjective (m./f.)` | La ville célèbre le centième anniversaire de sa fondation. *(The city celebrates the 100th anniversary of its founding.)* | centièmes | Nasal vowel [sɑ̃tjɛm]. |
| **le dernier / la dernière** | Last / Final | `luh dair-NYAY / lah dair-NYAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20dernier%20/%20la%20derni%C3%A8re&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | C'est le dernier jour pour déposer votre candidature. *(It's the last day to submit your application.)* | derniers / dernières | BANGS adjective: placed before the noun. Masculine silent 'r' [dɛʁnje]; feminine [dɛʁnjɛʁ]. |
| **la moitié** | Half (one half of something) | `lah mwah-TYAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20moiti%C3%A9&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Coupez la pomme en deux et donnez-moi la moitié. *(Cut the apple in two and give me half.)* | les moitiés | Feminine noun [mwatje]. 'Moitié' is a noun; 'demi' is an adjective. |
| **le demi / la demie** | Half / Half past | `luh duh-MEE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20demi%20/%20la%20demie&op=translate) | `Noun / Adjective (m./f.)` | Il est trois heures et demie. *(It is half past three.)* | un demi-litre, deux heures et demie | Agreement rule: before noun with hyphen, demi is invariable (une demi-heure); after noun, agrees in gender only (trois heures et demie). |
| **le quart** | Quarter / Fourth part / Quarter past/to | `luh KAHR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20quart&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Il est midi et quart. *(It is a quarter past twelve.)* | les quarts | Silent 't' [kaʁ]. 'Et quart' = quarter past; 'moins le quart' = quarter to. |

Ends in 's', invariable in plural: un tiers, deux tiers. |
| **le double** | Double / Twice the amount | `luh DOO-bluh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20double&op=translate) | `Masculine / Adjective` | Ce nouveau moteur offre le double de puissance. *(This new engine offers double the power.)* | les doubles | Masculine noun and adjective [dubl]. | **le triple** | Triple / Threefold | `luh TREE-pluh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20triple&op=translate) | `Masculine / Adjective` | Le prix du café a presque fait le triple en dix ans. *(The price of coffee has almost tripled in ten years.)* | les triples | Masculine noun and adjective [tʁipl]. |

| **la cinquantaine** | About fifty / In one's fifties | `lah sa(n)-kahn-TEN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20cinquantaine&op=translate) | `Feminine Collective (f.)` | Le conférencier a la cinquantaine bien sonnée. *(The lecturer is well into his fifties.)* | les cinquantaines | Nasal vowel [sɛ̃kɑ̃tɛn]. Followed by 'de'. |

| **le millier** | About a thousand / A thousand | `luh mee-LYAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20millier&op=translate) | `Masculine Collective (m.)` | Des milliers de touristes photographient le monument. *(Thousands of tourists photograph the monument.)* | les milliers | Masculine noun [milje]. Distinct from 'mille' (which is invariable). Milliers takes -s! |
| **le million** | Million (1,000,000) | `luh mee-LYOHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20million&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Paris compte plus de deux millions d'habitants intramuros. *(Paris has more than two million inhabitants within city limits.)* | les millions (takes -s in plural!) | Grammar rule: 'million' is a NOUN, so it DOES take an -s in plural: deux millions de personnes! |
| **le milliard** | Billion (1,000,000,000) | `luh mee-LYAHR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20milliard&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | La population mondiale dépasse huit milliards d'êtres humains. *(World population exceeds 8 billion human beings.)* | les milliards (takes -s in plural!) | Silent 'd' [miljaʁ]. Takes -s in plural and requires 'de' before nouns: 8 milliards de. |

Dual meaning: to count numbers and to intend (je compte partir demain). |
| **calculer** | To calculate / To compute | `kahl-kyoo-LAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=calculer&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group -er)` | Calculez mentalement la somme de ces deux montants. *(Calculate the sum of these two amounts in your head.)* | je calcule, tu calcules, il calcule, nous calculons, vous calculez, ils calculent | Regular -er verb [kalkyle]. Noun: la calculatrice (calculator). |
| **additionner** | To add up (math) / To add together | `ah-dee-syoh-NAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=additionner&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group -er)` | Si vous additionnez dix et quinze, vous obtenez vingt-cinq. *(If you add 10 and 15, you get 25.)* | j'additionne, nous additionnons | Doubles 'dd' and 'nn' [adisjɔne]. ## 4. Days of the Week and Months of the Year (Les jours et les mois)

| French (with Article) | English Meaning | Pronunciation | Audio | Gender / Word Type | Example Sentence | Plural / Conjugation | Liaison & Teacher's Notes |
|:---|:---|:---|:---:|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| **la quinzaine** | Fortnight / Two-week period | `lah ka(n)-ZEN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20quinzaine&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Le magazine paraît tous les quinze jours, c'est une quinzaine. *(The magazine appears every 15 days, it's a fortnightly.)* | les quinzaines | Suffix -aine indicates approx 15 days [kɛ̃zɛn]. Very common in French business schedules. |
| **le semestre** | Semester / Six-month term | `luh suh-MEST-ruh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20semestre&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | L'année universitaire est divisée en deux semestres d'études. *(The academic year is divided into two study semesters.)* | les semestres | Masculine noun [səmɛstʁ]. Latin sex (six) + mensis (month). |
| **le trimestre** | Quarter / Three-month term | `luh tree-MEST-ruh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20trimestre&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Le bulletin scolaire est envoyé aux parents à la fin de chaque trimestre. *(The report card is sent to parents at the end of each term.)* | les trimestres | Masculine noun [tʁimɛstʁ]. French school year is divided into 3 trimestres. |
| **le lendemain** | The following day / Next day | `luh lahnd-MA(N)` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20lendemain&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Le lendemain de la fête, tout le village nettoyait la place. *(The day after the party, the whole village was cleaning the square.)* | les lendemains | Nasal vowel [lɑ̃dmɛ̃]. Refers to the next day in narrative past/future. |
| **l'année (f.)** | Year (duration / subjective span) | `lah-NAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27ann%C3%A9e%20%28f.%29&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Bonne et heureuse année à vous et à vos proches ! *(Happy New Year to you and your loved ones!)* | les années | Distinction: 'an' is used with quantities (j'ai 25 ans); 'année' with adjectives (une belle année). |
| **l'an (m.)** | Year (calendar unit / age count) | `LAHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27an%20%28m.%29&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Nous nous réunissons une fois par an pour Noël. *(We gather once a year for Christmas.)* | les ans | Nasal vowel [ɑ̃]. Expression: le Nouvel An = New Year's Day. |
| **la veille** | The day before / The eve of | `lah VAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20veille&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Il prépare sa valise la veille de son départ pour l'Italie. *(He packs his suitcase the day before his departure for Italy.)* | les veilles | Liquid -eille [vɛj]. Expression: la veille de Noël = Christmas Eve. |
| **le surlendemain** | Two days later / Day after tomorrow (narrative) | `luh syoor-lahnd-MA(N)` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20surlendemain&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Le paquet est arrivé le surlendemain de la commande. *(The package arrived two days after the order.)* | les surlendemains | Prefix sur- + lendemain [syʁlɑ̃dmɛ̃]. |
| **l'avant-veille (f.)** | Two days before (the eve of the eve) | `lah-vahn-VAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27avant-veille%20%28f.%29&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Nous sommes arrivés l'avant-veille du mariage pour aider aux préparatifs. *(We arrived two days before the wedding to help with preparations.)* | les avant-veilles | Feminine noun [avɑ̃vɛj]. Useful temporal marker in narratives. |
| **le jour ouvrable** | Business day / Working day | `luh ZHOOR oo-VRAH-bluh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20jour%20ouvrable&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Le colis vous sera livré sous deux à trois jours ouvrables. *(The parcel will be delivered to you in 2 to 3 business days.)* | les jours ouvrables | Derived from ouvrir (to open) [uvʁabl]. Monday through Saturday (excluding holidays). |
| **le jour férié** | Public holiday / Bank holiday | `luh ZHOOR fay-ree-YAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20jour%20f%C3%A9ri%C3%A9&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Le premier mai est un jour férié chômé en France. *(May 1st is a non-working public holiday in France.)* | les jours fériés | France has 11 official public holidays (jours fériés) per year. |
| **bimensuel / bimensuelle** | Bi-monthly / Twice a month | `bee-mahn-SWEL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=bimensuel%20/%20bimensuelle&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Cette revue culturelle a une parution bimensuelle. *(This cultural magazine has a bi-monthly publication.)* | bimensuels / bimensuelles | Pattern -el -> -elle doubles 'll' [bimɑ̃sɥɛl]. Means twice a month (every 15 days). |
| **avant-hier** | The day before yesterday | `ah-vahn-TYAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=avant-hier&op=translate) | `Adverb of Time` | Le facteur a déposé cette lettre recommandée avant-hier. *(The mailman delivered this registered letter the day before yesterday.)* | Invariable adverb | Mandatory [t] liaison: avant‿hier [avɑ̃.t‿jɛʁ]. |
| **la ponctualité** | Punctuality | `lah pohnk-tyoo-ah-lee-TAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20ponctualit%C3%A9&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | La ponctualité est la politesse des rois, selon la formule célèbre. *(Punctuality is the politeness of kings, according to the famous saying.)* | les ponctualités | Nouns in -ité are feminine [pɔ̃ktɥalite]. |
| **ponctuel / ponctuelle** | Punctual / On-time | `pohnk-TYWEL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=ponctuel%20/%20ponctuelle&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Il est toujours très ponctuel à tous ses rendez-vous professionnels. *(He is always very punctual for all his business meetings.)* | ponctuels / ponctuelles | Pattern -el -> -elle: doubles 'll' in feminine [pɔ̃ktɥɛl]. |
| **après-demain** | The day after tomorrow | `ah-preh-duh-MA(N)` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=apr%C3%A8s-demain&op=translate) | `Adverb of Time` | Notre train part après-demain à la première heure. *(Our train leaves the day after tomorrow at the crack of dawn.)* | Invariable adverb | Hyphenated compound [apʁɛdəmɛ̃]. |
| **quotidien / quotidienne** | Daily / Everyday | `koh-tee-DYA(N) / koh-tee-DYEN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=quotidien%20/%20quotidienne&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | La marche à pied est une activité quotidienne excellente pour la santé. *(Walking is a daily activity excellent for health.)* | quotidiens / quotidiennes | Pattern -ien -> -ienne: doubles 'n' [kɔtidjɛ̃] / [kɔtidjɛn]. |
| **hebdomadaire** | Weekly | `eb-doh-mah-DAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=hebdomadaire&op=translate) | `Adjective / Noun (m./f.)` | Le journal hebdomadaire paraît tous les jeudis en kiosque. *(The weekly newspaper is published every Thursday at newsstands.)* | hebdomadaires | Silent 'h' [ɛbdɔmadɛʁ]. From Greek hebdomas (seven). Epicene ending in -aire. |
| **mensuel / mensuelle** | Monthly | `mahn-SWEL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=mensuel%20/%20mensuelle&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Je paie mon abonnement téléphonique par prélèvement mensuel. *(I pay my phone subscription by monthly direct debit.)* | mensuels / mensuelles | Pattern -el -> -elle: doubles 'll' in feminine [mɑ̃sɥɛl]. |
| **annuel / annuelle** | Annual / Yearly | `ah-NYWEL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=annuel%20/%20annuelle&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Le bilan financier annuel est présenté aux actionnaires. *(The annual financial balance sheet is presented to shareholders.)* | annuels / annuelles | Pattern -el -> -elle: doubles 'll' in feminine [anɥɛl]. |
| **le siècle** | Century (100 years) | `luh SYEH-kluh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20si%C3%A8cle&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Cette cathédrale gothique a été bâtie au treizième siècle. *(This Gothic cathedral was built in the 13th century.)* | les siècles | In French, centuries are always written with Roman numerals: le XXIe siècle [sjɛkl]. |
| **le millénaire** | Millennium (1,000 years) | `luh mee-lay-NAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20mill%C3%A9naire&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Le passage à l'an 2000 a marqué le début d'un nouveau millénaire. *(The turn of the year 2000 marked the start of a new millennium.)* | les millénaires | Double 'll' sounds [l] [milənɛʁ]. Masculine noun. |
| **la mi-janvier / mi-février** | Mid-January / Mid-February | `lah mee-zhahn-VYAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20mi-janvier%20/%20mi-f%C3%A9vrier&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Les soldes d'hiver débutent généralement vers la mi-janvier. *(The winter sales generally begin around mid-January.)* | la mi-mars, la mi-août | Prefix 'mi-' creates feminine compound nouns in French: la mi-juin, la mi-temps (halftime). |
| **au début du mois** | At the beginning of the month | `oh day-BYOO dyoo MWAH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=au%20d%C3%A9but%20du%20mois&op=translate) | `Temporal Phrase` | Le loyer doit être réglé au début du mois, avant le 5. *(The rent must be paid at the beginning of the month, before the 5th.)* | Opposite: à la fin du mois | Contraction de + le = du (du mois). |
| **à la fin du mois** | At the end of the month | `ah lah FA(N) dyoo MWAH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=%C3%A0%20la%20fin%20du%20mois&op=translate) | `Temporal Phrase` | Nous recevons notre bulletin de paie à la fin du mois. *(We receive our payslip at the end of the month.)* | Invariable temporal phrase | Nasal vowel in fin [fɛ̃]. Expression: 'joindre les deux bouts à la fin du mois' = to make ends meet. |
| **tous les jours** | Every day / Daily | `too lay ZHOOR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=tous%20les%20jours&op=translate) | `Frequency Expression` | Je bois un verre de jus d'orange pressé tous les jours. *(I drink a glass of fresh orange juice every day.)* | toutes les semaines, tous les mois | Silent 's' in tous when followed by noun: [tu le ʒuʁ]. (Pronounced [tus] only as standalone pronoun!). |
| **chaque** | Each / Every (singular) | `SHAHK` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=chaque&op=translate) | `Indefinite Adjective (invariable)` | Chaque élève reçoit un manuel de français au début de l'année. *(Each student receives a French textbook at the start of the year.)* | Always followed by singular noun! | Grammar rule: 'chaque' is strictly singular (chaque jour, chaque matin). Never add -s! |
| **s'écouler** | To elapse / To slip by (time) | `say-koo-LAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=s%27%C3%A9couler&op=translate) | `Reflexive Verb (1st group)` | Les semaines s'écoulent à toute vitesse quand on est passionné. *(Weeks slip by at top speed when one is passionate.)* | il s'écoule, ils s'écoulent | Regular -er verb [ekule]. Used for time flowing or water trickling. |
| **se terminer** | To end / To come to a close / To finish | `suh tair-mee-NAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=se%20terminer&op=translate) | `Reflexive Verb (1st group)` | Le cours de mathématiques se termine à seize heures trente. *(The math class ends at 4:30 PM.)* | il se termine, ils se terminent | Regular -er verb [tɛʁmine]. Synonym of 'se finir'. |
| **se dérouler** | To take place / To unfold / To happen | `suh day-roo-LAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=se%20d%C3%A9rouler&op=translate) | `Reflexive Verb (1st group)` | Le festival de musique se déroule tout au long du mois de juillet. *(The music festival takes place throughout the month of July.)* | il se déroule, ils se déroulent | Elegant synonym of 'avoir lieu' (to take place) [deʁule]. |

## 5. Telling the Time (Dire l'heure)

| French (with Article) | English Meaning | Pronunciation | Audio | Gender / Word Type | Example Sentence | Plural / Conjugation | Liaison & Teacher's Notes |
|:---|:---|:---|:---:|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| **le chronomètre** | Stopwatch / Timer | `luh kroh-noh-MET-ruh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20chronom%C3%A8tre&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | L'entraîneur déclenche son chronomètre au coup de sifflet. *(The coach starts his stopwatch at the whistle blow.)* | les chronomètres | 'ch' pronounced [k]: [kʁɔnɔmɛtʁ]. Masculine noun. |
| **le sablier** | Hourglass / Sand timer | `luh sah-blee-YAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20sablier&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Retournez le sablier de trois minutes pour cuire l'œuf à la coque. *(Turn over the 3-minute sand timer to boil the soft-boiled egg.)* | les sabliers | Silent 'r' [sablije]. Derived from sable (sand). |
| **la sonnerie** | Alarm ringtone / Bell chime / School bell | `lah sohn-REE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20sonnerie&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | La sonnerie stridente du collège annonce la fin de la récréation. *(The shrill school bell announces the end of recess.)* | les sonneries | Doubles 'nn' [sɔnʁi]. Phone ringtone is also 'la sonnerie'. |
| **le cadran** | Clock face / Dial | `luh kah-DRAHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20cadran&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Les chiffres dorés se détachent nettement sur le cadran noir. *(The golden numerals stand out clearly on the black dial.)* | les cadrans | Silent 'n' in nasal vowel [kadʁɑ̃]. Cadran solaire = sundial. |
| **l'aiguille (f.)** | Clock hand / Sewing needle | `lay-GWEE-yuh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27aiguille%20%28f.%29&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | La grande aiguille indique les minutes et la petite indique les heures. *(The big hand shows minutes and the small hand shows hours.)* | les aiguilles | Pronounced with [ɡɥi] sound [eɡɥij]. Dual meaning: clock hand and medical/sewing needle. |
| **la grande aiguille** | Minute hand (of clock) | `lah GRAHND ay-GWEE-yuh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20grande%20aiguille&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Quand la grande aiguille est sur le 12, c'est l'heure pile. *(When the minute hand is on the 12, it is the exact hour.)* | les grandes aiguilles | Adjective grand placed before noun [ɡʁɑ̃d eɡɥij]. |
| **la petite aiguille** | Hour hand (of clock) | `lah puh-TEET ay-GWEE-yuh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20petite%20aiguille&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | La petite aiguille pointe vers le chiffre sept. *(The hour hand points towards the numeral seven.)* | les petites aiguilles | Adjective petit placed before noun [pətit eɡɥij]. |
| **la trotteuse** | Second hand (sweeping hand of clock) | `lah troh-TUHZ` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20trotteuse&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | La trotteuse rouge avance de seconde en seconde sans s'arrêter. *(The red second hand ticks second by second without stopping.)* | les trotteuses | Derived from trotter (to trot) [tʁɔtøz]. Feminine noun. |
| **la demi-heure** | Half hour / Thirty minutes | `lah duh-mee-UHR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20demi-heure&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Le train a une demi-heure de retard à cause d'une panne. *(The train is a half hour late because of a breakdown.)* | les demi-heures | Grammar rule: with hyphen before noun, 'demi' is invariable (demi-heure). |
| **le quart d'heure** | Quarter of an hour / 15 minutes | `luh KAHR DUHR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20quart%20d%27heure&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | J'arrive dans un quart d'heure, prépare le café ! *(I arrive in 15 minutes, get the coffee ready!)* | les quarts d'heure | Cultural term: 'le quart d'heure de politesse' = arriving 15 minutes late to a dinner party is considered polite in France! |
| **le retardataire / la retardataire** | Latecomer / Person who is late | `luh ruh-tar-dah-TAIR / lah ruh-tar-dah-TAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20retardataire%20/%20la%20retardataire&op=translate) | `Noun (m./f.)` | Les retardataires doivent attendre la pause pour entrer dans la salle de spectacle. *(Latecomers must wait for the intermission to enter the theater.)* | les retardataires | Epicene ending in -aire [ʁətaʁdatɛʁ]. |
| **le temps libre** | Free time / Leisure time | `luh TAHN LEE-bruh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20temps%20libre&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Que fais-tu de ton temps libre le week-end ? *(What do you do with your free time on weekends?)* | les temps libres | Silent 'ps' in temps [tɑ̃ libʁ]. Synonym of 'loisirs'. |
| **presser le pas** | To quicken one's pace / To hurry up | `preh-SAY luh PAH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=presser%20le%20pas&op=translate) | `Idiomatic Expression` | Il presse le pas pour attraper son train qui part dans cinq minutes. *(He quickens his pace to catch his train leaving in 5 minutes.)* | je presse le pas, nous pressons le pas | Silent 's' in pas [pa]. Doubled 'ss' in presser [pʁese]. |
| **être à l'heure** | To be on time / To be punctual | `EH-truh ah LUHR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=%C3%AAtre%20%C3%A0%20l%27heure&op=translate) | `Verbal Locution` | Ce train de banlieue est rarement à l'heure le matin. *(This commuter train is rarely on time in the morning.)* | je suis à l'heure, nous sommes à l'heure | Conjugation of être. |
| **avoir du retard** | To be running late / To have a delay | `ah-VWAHR dyoo ruh-TAHR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=avoir%20du%20retard&op=translate) | `Verbal Locution` | Le vol en provenance de Londres a quarante minutes de retard. *(The flight from London has a 40-minute delay.)* | j'ai du retard, nous avons du retard | Used for public transport delays and people running late. |
| **avoir de l'avance** | To be ahead of time / To be early | `ah-VWAHR duh lah-VAHNSS` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=avoir%20de%20l%27avance&op=translate) | `Verbal Locution` | Nous avons une demi-heure d'avance sur notre horaire prévu. *(We are 30 minutes ahead of our scheduled timetable.)* | j'ai de l'avance, nous avons de l'avance | Liaison: d'avance [davɑ̃s]. |
| **avancer** | To be fast (clock) / To set forward | `ah-vahn-SAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=avancer&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group -cer)` | Ma montre avance de cinq minutes chaque jour. *(My watch is five minutes fast every day.)* | j'avance, nous avançons (cedilla ç), vous avancez | Takes cedilla 'ç' before 'o' in nous avançons [avɑ̃sɔ̃]. Dual meaning: move forward and clock running fast. |
| **retarder** | To be slow (clock) / To delay | `ruh-tar-DAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=retarder&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group -er)` | Cette vieille horloge retarde d'un quart d'heure. *(This old clock is running 15 minutes slow.)* | elle retarde, elles retardent | Regular -er verb [ʁətaʁde]. Opposite of avancer. |
| **l'heure d'été (f.)** | Daylight Saving Time / Summer time | `LUHR day-TAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27heure%20d%27%C3%A9t%C3%A9%20%28f.%29&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | En mars, nous passons à l'heure d'été en avançant d'une heure. *(In March, we switch to Daylight Saving Time by moving forward one hour.)* | Preposition: à l'heure d'été | France switches to heure d'été (UTC+2) on the last Sunday of March. |
| **l'heure d'hiver (f.)** | Standard Time / Winter time | `LUHR dee-VAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27heure%20d%27hiver%20%28f.%29&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | À l'heure d'hiver, on gagne une heure de sommeil en octobre. *(In winter time, we gain one hour of sleep in October.)* | Preposition: à l'heure d'hiver | France switches back to heure d'hiver (UTC+1) on the last Sunday of October. |
| **le fuseau horaire** | Time zone | `luh fyoo-ZOH zoh-RAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20fuseau%20horaire&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Paris et Londres sont sur deux fuseaux horaires différents. *(Paris and London are in two different time zones.)* | les fuseaux horaires (takes -x) | Words ending in -eau take -x in plural: un fuseau -> des fuseaux [fyzo]. |
| **le décalage horaire** | Jet lag / Time difference | `luh day-kah-LAHZH zoh-RAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20d%C3%A9calage%20horaire&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Après un vol transatlantique, je ressens le décalage horaire. *(After a transatlantic flight, I feel the jet lag.)* | les décalages horaires | Silent 'h' in horaire: mandatory [z] liaison décalage‿horaire [dekalaʒ ɔʁɛʁ]. |
| **l'horaire (m.)** | Schedule / Timetable / Hours | `loh-RAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27horaire%20%28m.%29&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Consultez les horaires de bus sur l'application mobile. *(Check the bus timetable on the mobile app.)* | les horaires | Masculine noun [ɔʁɛʁ]. Liaison: un‿horaire [œ̃.n‿ɔʁɛʁ]. |
| **prendre son temps** | To take one's time / Not rush | `PRAHN-druh sohn TAHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=prendre%20son%20temps&op=translate) | `Idiomatic Expression` | Prends tout ton temps pour savourer ce bon repas. *(Take all your time to savor this good meal.)* | je prends mon temps, nous prenons notre temps | Irregular verb prendre [pʁɑ̃dʁ]. Opposite: se presser / se dépêcher. |
| **passer du temps** | To spend time | `pah-SAY dyoo TAHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=passer%20du%20temps&op=translate) | `Verbal Locution` | J'aime passer du temps en famille le dimanche. *(I like spending time with family on Sundays.)* | je passe du temps, nous passons du temps | Distinction: passer du temps = spend time; dépenser de l'argent = spend money (never *dépenser du temps!). |
| **perdre son temps** | To waste one's time | `PAIR-druh sohn TAHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=perdre%20son%20temps&op=translate) | `Idiomatic Expression` | Ne perds pas ton temps sur les réseaux sociaux, travaille ! *(Don't waste your time on social media, work!)* | je perds mon temps, nous perdons notre temps | Verb perdre [pɛʁdʁ]. Opposite: gagner du temps (to save time). |
| **le créneau horaire** | Time slot / Window of time | `luh kray-NOH zoh-RAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20cr%C3%A9neau%20horaire&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | J'ai réservé un créneau horaire entre 14h et 16h pour la livraison. *(I booked a time slot between 2:00 and 4:00 PM for delivery.)* | les créneaux horaires (takes -x) | Words ending in -eau take -x in plural: créneaux [kʁeno]. Mandatory [z] liaison créneau‿horaire. |
| **être pressé / pressée** | To be in a hurry / rushed | `EH-truh preh-SAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=%C3%AAtre%20press%C3%A9%20/%20press%C3%A9e&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Dépêche-toi, je suis très pressé, le train va partir ! *(Hurry up, I'm in a big hurry, the train is leaving!)* | pressés / pressées | Pronounced identically m./f. [pʁese]. Doubles 'ss'. |
| **avoir le temps** | To have time | `ah-VWAHR luh TAHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=avoir%20le%20temps&op=translate) | `Verbal Locution` | As-tu le temps de boire un café avec moi avant la réunion ? *(Do you have time to drink a coffee with me before the meeting?)* | j'ai le temps, nous avons le temps | Negative: je n'ai pas le temps (I don't have time). |
| **manquer de temps** | To lack time / To be short on time | `mahn-KAY duh TAHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=manquer%20de%20temps&op=translate) | `Verbal Locution` | Nous manquons de temps pour achever ce projet aujourd'hui. *(We are short on time to finish this project today.)* | je manque de temps, nous manquons de temps | Regular -er verb manquer [mɑ̃ke]. Preposition: manquer de. |

| French (with Article) | English Meaning | Pronunciation | Audio | Gender / Word Type | Example Sentence | Plural / Conjugation | Liaison & Teacher's Notes |
|:---|:---|:---|:---:|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| **le baromètre** | Barometer (air pressure instrument) | `luh bah-roh-MET-ruh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20barom%C3%A8tre&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | La baisse rapide du baromètre annonce l'arrivée imminente d'une tempête. *(The rapid drop of the barometer announces the imminent arrival of a storm.)* | les baromètres | Masculine noun [baʁɔmɛtʁ]. Figurative: baromètre politique. |
| **le bulletin météo** | Weather bulletin / Forecast segment | `luh bool-TA(N) may-tay-OH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20bulletin%20m%C3%A9t%C3%A9o&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Le présentateur annonce de la pluie au bulletin météo de 20h. *(The presenter announces rain on the 8:00 PM weather report.)* | les bulletins météo | Nasal vowel [byltɛ̃]. Météo remains invariable in this compound. |
| **le thermomètre** | Thermometer | `luh tair-moh-MET-ruh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20thermom%C3%A8tre&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Le thermomètre extérieur indique quinze degrés au lever du soleil. *(The outdoor thermometer reads 15 degrees at sunrise.)* | les thermomètres | Silent 'h' [tɛʁmɔmɛtʁ]. Device for measuring temperature. |
| **le degré Celsius** | Degree Celsius | `luh duh-GRAY sel-SYOOSS` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20degr%C3%A9%20Celsius&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Il fait zéro degré Celsius, attention au verglas sur la route. *(It is zero degrees Celsius, watch out for black ice on the road.)* | les degrés Celsius | Final 's' in Celsius IS sounded: [sɛlsjys]. Zero is singular in French: zéro degré (no -s)! |
| **le ciel bleu** | Blue sky / Clear sky | `luh SYEL BLUH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20ciel%20bleu&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Nous nous réveillons sous un magnifique ciel bleu sans nuages. *(We wake up under a magnificent cloudless blue sky.)* | les ciels bleus (or cieux) | Bleu takes regular -s in plural: bleus (not -x)! [sjɛl blø]. |
| **l'éclaircie (f.)** | Sunny interval / Break in clouds / Bright spell | `lay-klair-SEE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27%C3%A9claircie%20%28f.%29&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Le bulletin météo prévoit de belles éclaircies pour l'après-midi. *(The forecast predicts fine sunny intervals for the afternoon.)* | les éclaircies | Feminine noun [eklɛʁsi]. Often used in plural in weather reports: des éclaircies. |
| **la buée** | Condensation / Fog on glass / Steam | `lah byoo-AY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20bu%C3%A9e&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Essuie la buée sur les vitres de la voiture pour mieux voir la route. *(Wipe the condensation on the car windows to see the road better.)* | les buées | Feminine noun [bɥe]. Adjective: embué / embuée. |
| **la brume** | Haze / Light mist | `lah BREWM` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20brume&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | La brume matinale se dissipe lentement avec le lever du soleil. *(The morning mist slowly dissipates with sunrise.)* | les brumes | Lighter than brouillard [bʁym]. Adjective: brumeux / brumeuse. |
| **l'orage (m.)** | Thunderstorm / Storm | `loh-RAHZH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27orage%20%28m.%29&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Un violent orage d'été a éclaté en fin d'après-midi. *(A violent summer thunderstorm broke out in late afternoon.)* | les orages | Nouns in -age are masculine [ɔʁaʒ]. Adjective: orageux (stormy). |
| **l'éclair (m.)** | Lightning flash / Lightning bolt | `lay-KLAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27%C3%A9clair%20%28m.%29&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Un éclair lumineux a déchiré le ciel sombre avant le tonnerre. *(A bright flash of lightning ripped through the dark sky before the thunder.)* | les éclairs | Masculine noun [eklɛʁ]. Dual meaning: lightning bolt and delicious cream-filled pastry! |
| **le coup de vent** | Gust of wind / Sudden blast of wind | `luh KOO duh VAHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20coup%20de%20vent&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Un brusque coup de vent a fait claquer la porte du jardin. *(A sudden gust of wind slammed the garden door shut.)* | les coups de vent | Silent 'p' in coup [ku]. Silent 't' in vent [vɑ̃]. |
| **la tempête** | Storm / Gale / Tempest | `lah tahn-PET` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20temp%C3%AAte&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | La tempête hivernale a déraciné plusieurs arbres sur la côte. *(The winter storm uprooted several trees on the coast.)* | les tempêtes | Circumflex on 'ê' [tɑ̃pɛt]. Feminine noun. |
| **l'averse (f.)** | Downpour / Sudden shower / Rain squall | `lah-VAIRSS` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27averse%20%28f.%29&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Nous nous sommes abrités sous un porche pendant l'averse. *(We sheltered under a porch during the downpour.)* | les averses | Feminine noun [avɛʁs]. Une averse passagère = a passing shower. |
| **la goutte de pluie** | Raindrop | `lah GOOT duh PLWEE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20goutte%20de%20pluie&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Je sens les premières gouttes de pluie sur mon visage. *(I feel the first raindrops on my face.)* | les gouttes de pluie | Doubles 'tt' in goutte [ɡut]. Plural: des gouttes de pluie. |
| **le flocon de neige** | Snowflake | `luh floh-KOHN duh NEZH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20flocon%20de%20neige&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | De gros flocons de neige tombent doucement sur le jardin. *(Big snowflakes are falling gently on the garden.)* | les flocons de neige | Masculine noun [flɔkɔ̃]. Nasal vowel. |
| **la grêle** | Hail / Hailstones | `lah GREL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20gr%C3%AAle&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | L'orage a apporté de la grêle qui a abîmé les vignes. *(The storm brought hail that damaged the vineyards.)* | les grêles | Circumflex on 'ê' [ɡʁɛl]. Verb: grêler (il grêle = it is hailing). |
| **le verglas** | Black ice / Glaze of ice on road | `luh vair-GLAH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20verglas&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Roulez prudemment, la chaussée est couverte de verglas. *(Drive carefully, the road is covered in black ice.)* | les verglas (invariable in plural) | Silent 's' [vɛʁɡla]. Ends in 's', invariable in plural. Major winter driving hazard in France. |
| **le gel** | Frost / Freeze | `luh ZHEL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20gel&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Le gel printanier est redouté par les arboriculteurs. *(Spring frost is dreaded by fruit growers.)* | les gels | Sounded 'l' [ʒɛl]. Verb: geler (to freeze: il gèle). |
| **le dégel** | Thaw / Melting of snow and ice | `luh day-ZHEL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20d%C3%A9gel&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Avec le dégel, le niveau des rivières monte rapidement. *(With the thaw, river levels rise rapidly.)* | les dégels | Prefix dé- indicates reversal of gel [deʒɛl]. |
| **la canicule** | Heatwave / Scorcher period | `lah kah-nee-KYOOL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20canicule&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Pendant la canicule estivale, buvez beaucoup d'eau fraîche. *(During the summer heatwave, drink plenty of fresh water.)* | les canicules | Feminine noun [kanikyl]. France has an official national 'Plan Canicule' alert system. |
| **l'ombre (f.)** | Shade / Shadow | `LOHN-bruh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27ombre%20%28f.%29&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Asseyons-nous à l'ombre de ce grand platane. *(Let's sit in the shade of this big plane tree.)* | les ombres | Feminine noun [ɔ̃bʁ]. Expression: à l'ombre = in the shade. |
| **la brise** | Breeze / Gentle wind | `lah BREEZ` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20brise&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Une légère brise marine rafraîchit la plage en fin d'après-midi. *(A light sea breeze cools the beach in late afternoon.)* | les brises | Single 's' pronounced [z] [bʁiz]. Feminine noun. |
| **la météo est clémente** | The weather is mild / pleasant | `lah may-tay-OH eh klay-MAHNT` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20m%C3%A9t%C3%A9o%20est%20cl%C3%A9mente&op=translate) | `Idiomatic Phrase` | La météo est très clémente pour notre week-end de randonnée. *(The weather is very mild and pleasant for our hiking weekend.)* | Adjective clément agrees with météo | Clément / clémente means mild, clement, forgiving [klemɑ̃t]. |
| **il fait frais** | It is cool / chilly | `eel feh FREH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=il%20fait%20frais&op=translate) | `Impersonal Weather Expression` | Mets une petite veste ce soir, il fait un peu frais. *(Put on a light jacket tonight, it's a bit chilly.)* | Impersonal il fait | Silent 's' in frais [fʁɛ]. Feminine adjective: fraîche [fʁɛʃ]. Weather takes impersonal 'il fait'. |
| **il fait lourd** | It is humid / muggy / close | `eel feh LOOR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=il%20fait%20lourd&op=translate) | `Impersonal Weather Expression` | L'atmosphère est étouffante, il fait lourd avant l'orage. *(The atmosphere is stifling, it's muggy before the storm.)* | Impersonal il fait | Silent 'd' [luʁ]. Describes heavy, humid pre-storm weather. |
| **il gèle** | It is freezing / It freezes | `eel ZHEL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=il%20g%C3%A8le&op=translate) | `Impersonal Verb geler` | Attention sur les ponts, il gèle à pierre fendre ce matin ! *(Watch out on bridges, it's freezing intensely this morning!)* | Impersonal verb (only used with il) | Stem changer: 'e' becomes 'è' in il gèle [ʒɛl]. Expression: geler à pierre fendre = freeze hard. |
| **il pleut à verse** | It is pouring rain / raining cats and dogs | `eel pluh ah VAIRSS` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=il%20pleut%20%C3%A0%20verse&op=translate) | `Idiomatic Weather Expression` | Prends un grand parapluie, il pleut à verse dehors ! *(Take a big umbrella, it's pouring rain outside!)* | Synonym: il pleut des cordes | Vivid French idiom: pleuvoir à verse [plø a vɛʁs] or pleuvoir des cordes (literally 'raining ropes')! |
| **fondre** | To melt / To thaw | `FOHN-druh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=fondre&op=translate) | `Verb (3rd group)` | La neige fond rapidement sous les premiers rayons du soleil. *(The snow melts quickly under the first rays of the sun.)* | je fonds, tu fonds, il fond, nous fondons, vous fondez, ils fondent | Silent 'd' in singular: il fond [fɔ̃]. Nasal vowel. |
| **blanchir** | To whiten / To become covered in snow | `blahn-SHEER` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=blanchir&op=translate) | `Verb (2nd group -ir)` | La neige commence à blanchir les toits du village. *(The snow is starting to whiten the roofs of the village.)* | je blanchis, tu blanchis, il blanchit, nous blanchissons, vous blanchissez, ils blanchissent | 2nd group regular verb: nous blanchissons [blɑ̃ʃisɔ̃]. From blanc (white). |
| **se couvrir** | To cloud over (sky) / To dress warmly | `suh koo-VREER` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=se%20couvrir&op=translate) | `Reflexive Verb (3rd group)` | Le ciel se couvre de nuages gris, la pluie approche. *(The sky is clouding over with gray clouds, rain is coming.)* | il se couvre, ils se couvrent | Dual meaning: sky getting overcast (le ciel se couvre) and putting on warm clothes (couvre-toi bien !). |
