## 7. Immediate Family Members (La famille proche)

| French (with Article) | English Meaning | Pronunciation | Audio | Gender / Word Type | Example Sentence | Plural / Conjugation | Liaison & Teacher's Notes |
|:---|:---|:---|:---:|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| **le conjoint / la conjointe** | Spouse / Partner (legal status) | `luh kohn-ZHWA(N) / lah kohn-ZHWANT` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20conjoint%20/%20la%20conjointe&op=translate) | `Noun (m./f.)` | Le conjoint survivant hérite du logement familial. *(The surviving spouse inherits the family home.)* | les conjoints / les conjointes | Masculine silent 't' [kɔ̃ʒwɛ̃]; feminine sounds 't' [kɔ̃ʒwɑ̃t]. Formal administrative term. |
| **le compagnon / la compagne** | Life partner / Partner (unmarried) | `luh kohn-pah-NYOHN / lah kohn-PAH-nyuh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20compagnon%20/%20la%20compagne&op=translate) | `Noun (m./f.)` | Elle vit avec son compagnon depuis cinq ans. *(She has lived with her partner for five years.)* | les compagnons / les compagnes | Palatal gn [ɲ] [kɔ̃paɲɔ̃] / [kɔ̃paɲ]. Common term for cohabiting couples. |
| **le beau-parent** | Stepparent / Parent-in-law (general) | `luh boh-pah-RAHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20beau-parent&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | La relation avec un beau-parent demande de l'écoute et du respect mutuel. *(The relationship with a stepparent requires listening and mutual respect.)* | les beaux-parents (takes -x) | Plural: les beaux-parents = in-laws or stepparents [bopaʁɑ̃]. |
| **la belle-famille** | The in-laws / Family by marriage | `lah bel-fah-MEEY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20belle-famille&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Nous passons le réveillon de Noël dans ma belle-famille en Bretagne. *(We are spending Christmas Eve with my in-laws in Brittany.)* | les belles-familles | Liquid -ille [bɛlfamij]. Collective noun covering all of one's spouse's family. |
| **le beau-fils / le gendre** | Son-in-law / Stepson | `luh boh-FEESS / luh ZHAHN-druh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20beau-fils%20/%20le%20gendre&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Leur gendre est avocat et s'entend très bien avec la famille. *(Their son-in-law is a lawyer and gets on very well with the family.)* | les beaux-fils / les gendres | Sounded 's' in fils [fis]. 'Gendre' specifically means son-in-law; 'beau-fils' can be stepson or son-in-law. |
| **la belle-fille / la bru** | Daughter-in-law / Stepdaughter | `lah bel-FEEY / lah BREW` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20belle-fille%20/%20la%20bru&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Ma belle-fille attend un heureux événement pour le printemps. *(My daughter-in-law is expecting a baby for spring.)* | les belles-filles / les brus | 'Bru' [bʁy] is traditional for son's wife (daughter-in-law); 'belle-fille' covers both. |
| **le lien de parenté** | Family connection / Kinship tie / Kin relationship | `luh LYA(N) duh pah-rahn-TAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20lien%20de%20parent%C3%A9&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Quel est votre lien de parenté avec le défunt ? *(What is your family connection with the deceased?)* | les liens de parenté | Nasal [ljɛ̃]. Formal administrative question on official records. |
| **le clan familial** | Family clan / Extended family group | `luh KLAHN fah-mee-YAHL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20clan%20familial&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Tout le clan familial se réunit dans la grande maison de vacances chaque été. *(The whole family clan gathers in the large holiday home each summer.)* | les clans familiaux (irregular -aux) | Adjective familial -> familiaux takes -aux in plural [klɑ̃ familjo]. |
| **le demi-frère** | Half-brother / Stepbrother | `luh duh-mee-FRAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20demi-fr%C3%A8re&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Mon demi-frère et moi partageons le même père. *(My half-brother and I share the same father.)* | les demi-frères | 'Demi' is invariable with hyphen before noun: des demi-frères [dəmifʁɛʁ]. |
| **la demi-sœur** | Half-sister / Stepsister | `lah duh-mee-SUHR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20demi-s%C5%93ur&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Ma demi-sœur cadette entre au collège cette année. *(My younger half-sister enters middle school this year.)* | les demi-sœurs | Plural: des demi-sœurs [dəmisœʁ]. |
| **les aïeux (m. pl.)** | Ancestors / Forefathers | `lay zah-YUH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=les%20a%C3%AFeux%20%28m.%20pl.%29&op=translate) | `Masculine Plural (m. pl.)` | Nos aïeux ont bâti cette maison en pierre de taille. *(Our ancestors built this house of ashlar stone.)* | Irregular plural of aïeul -> aïeux | Mandatory [z] liaison: nos‿aïeux [no-z-ajø]. Trema on 'ï'. |
| **l'arrière-grand-père (m.)** | Great-grandfather | `lah-ryair-grahn-PAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27arri%C3%A8re-grand-p%C3%A8re%20%28m.%29&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Mon arrière-grand-père a fêté son centième anniversaire. *(My great-grandfather celebrated his 100th birthday.)* | les arrière-grands-pères | Prefix arrière- remains invariable; grand and père take -s in plural. |
| **l'arrière-grand-mère (f.)** | Great-grandmother | `lah-ryair-grahn-MAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27arri%C3%A8re-grand-m%C3%A8re%20%28f.%29&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Mon arrière-grand-mère me racontait des contes d'autrefois. *(My great-grandmother used to tell me tales of long ago.)* | les arrière-grands-mères | Notice 'grand' traditionally takes -s without 'e' in grands-mères. |
| **le parrain** | Godfather | `luh pah-RA(N)` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20parrain&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Mon parrain m'a offert une belle montre pour ma communion. *(My godfather gave me a nice watch for my communion.)* | les parrains | Nasal vowel [paʁɛ̃]. Doubles 'rr'. Also means mafia boss (The Godfather = Le Parrain). |
| **la marraine** | Godmother | `lah mah-REN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20marraine&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Ma marraine m'envoie toujours une carte postale pour ma fête. *(My godmother always sends me a postcard for my name day.)* | les marraines | Doubles 'rr' [maʁɛn]. Fairy godmother = la fée marraine. |
| **le filleul** | Godson | `luh fee-YUL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20filleul&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | J'emmène mon filleul au zoo pour son anniversaire. *(I am taking my godson to the zoo for his birthday.)* | les filleuls | Liquid -ill- [fijœl]. Masculine noun. |
| **la filleule** | Goddaughter | `lah fee-YUL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20filleule&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Sa filleule fait de brillantes études de médecine. *(His goddaughter is doing brilliant medical studies.)* | les filleules | Feminine adds silent '-e' [fijœl]. |
| **le tonton** | Uncle (informal / affectionate childish) | `luh tohn-TOHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20tonton&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Tonton Pierre nous emmène faire du vélo dimanche. *(Uncle Pierre is taking us cycling on Sunday.)* | les tontons | Affectionate reduplication of oncle [tɔ̃tɔ̃]. |
| **la tatie / la tata** | Auntie (informal / affectionate childish) | `lah tah-TEE / lah tah-TAH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20tatie%20/%20la%20tata&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Tatie Monique prépare les meilleures tartes aux pommes. *(Auntie Monique bakes the best apple pies.)* | les taties / les tatas | Affectionate reduplication of tante [tati] / [tata]. |
| **le papy / le papi** | Grandpa / Grandad (affectionate) | `luh pah-PEE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20papy%20/%20le%20papi&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Papi raconte toujours des histoires passionnantes aux enfants. *(Grandpa always tells exciting stories to the children.)* | les papys / les papis | Universal everyday affectionate term in France [papi]. |
| **la mamie / la mamy** | Grandma / Granny (affectionate) | `lah mah-MEE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20mamie%20/%20la%20mamy&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Mamie tricote une écharpe en laine pour l'hiver. *(Granny is knitting a wool scarf for winter.)* | les mamies / les mamys | Universal everyday affectionate term in France [mami]. |
| **la fratrie** | Siblings (all brothers & sisters collectively) | `lah frah-TREE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20fratrie&op=translate) | `Feminine Collective (f.)` | Dans notre fratrie de quatre, je suis le plus âgé. *(In our sibling group of four, I am the oldest.)* | les fratries | Collective noun [fʁatʁi]. Covers all brothers and sisters of a family. |
| **la parenté** | Kinship / Relatives / Relationship by blood | `lah pah-rahn-TAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20parent%C3%A9&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Toute la parenté s'est rassemblée pour fêter les noces d'or. *(All the relatives gathered to celebrate the golden wedding anniversary.)* | les parentés | Nouns ending in -té are feminine [paʁɑ̃te]. |
| **les proches (m. pl.)** | Loved ones / Close relatives and friends | `lay PROHSH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=les%20proches%20%28m.%20pl.%29&op=translate) | `Plural Noun (m. pl.)` | Il a réuni ses proches pour un dîner chaleureux. *(He gathered his loved ones for a warm dinner.)* | un proche -> des proches | Covers immediate family and dearest intimate friends [pʁɔʃ]. |
| **le foyer** | Household / Family home / Hearth | `luh fwah-YAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20foyer&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Ce foyer fiscal compte deux adultes et trois enfants à charge. *(This tax household counts two adults and three dependent children.)* | les foyers | Liquid -oy- [fwaje]. Dual meaning: family hearth/home and tax household. |
| **adopter** | To adopt (a child / pet) | `ah-dohp-TAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=adopter&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group -er)` | Ils ont adopté un petit garçon originaire de Colombie. *(They adopted a little boy from Colombia.)* | j'adopte, tu adoptes, il adopte, nous adoptons, vous adoptez, ils adoptent | Regular -er verb [adɔpte]. Noun: l'adoption (f.). |
| **choyer** | To pamper / To cherish / To cosset | `shwah-YAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=choyer&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group stem-changing -yer)` | Les grands-parents choient leurs petits-enfants pendant les vacances. *(The grandparents pamper their grandchildren during holidays.)* | je choie, tu choies, il choie, nous choyons, vous choyez, ils choient | Stem changer: 'y' becomes 'i' before silent endings (je choie [ʃwa], nous choyons [ʃwajɔ̃]). |
| **hériter de** | To inherit from | `ay-ree-TAY duh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=h%C3%A9riter%20de&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group -er)` | Elle a hérité d'une belle maison ancienne de sa grand-mère. *(She inherited a beautiful old house from her grandmother.)* | j'hérite de, nous héritons de | Silent 'h' [eʁite]. Requires preposition 'de' (hériter de quelqu'un/quelque chose). |
| **fonder une famille** | To start a family | `fohn-DAY oon fah-MEEY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=fonder%20une%20famille&op=translate) | `Expression / Verb fonder` | Ils souhaitent acheter une maison avant de fonder une famille. *(They wish to buy a house before starting a family.)* | je fonde une famille, nous fondons une famille | Liquid -ille in famille [famij]. Regular verb fonder [fɔ̃de]. |
| **divorcer** | To get divorced | `dee-vor-SAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=divorcer&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group -cer)` | Après de longues discussions, ils ont décidé de divorcer à l'amiable. *(After lengthy discussions, they decided on an amicable divorce.)* | je divorce, nous divorçons (cedilla ç), vous divorcez | Takes cedilla 'ç' before 'o' in nous divorçons [divɔʁsɔ̃]. Noun: le divorce. |

Cedilla ç ensures [s] sound. |
| **la teinte** | Tint / Tone / Color cast | `lah TA(N)T` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20teinte&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | J'adore cette teinte chaude de terracotta pour le salon. *(I love this warm terracotta tint for the living room.)* | les teintes | Nasal vowel [tɛ̃t]. Verb: teinter (to tint). |
| **la palette de couleurs** | Color palette | `lah pah-LET duh koo-LUHR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20palette%20de%20couleurs&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | L'artiste utilise une palette de couleurs pastel très douces. *(The artist uses a very soft pastel color palette.)* | les palettes de couleurs | Doubles 'tt' in palette [palɛt]. |
| **beige** | Beige / Sand-colored | `BEHZH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=beige&op=translate) | `Adjective (invariable in gender)` | Il porte un trench-coat beige très élégant. *(He wears a very elegant beige trench coat.)* | beiges (takes -s in plural) | Epicene ending in -e [bɛʒ]. Takes -s in plural: des manteaux beiges. |
| **turquoise** | Turquoise | `toor-KWAHZ` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=turquoise&op=translate) | `Adjective (invariable)` | Les eaux turquoise de cette crique sont transparentes. *(The turquoise waters of this cove are crystal clear.)* | Invariable (never adds -s or -e) | Grammar rule: color derived from a gemstone/mineral is strictly INVARIABLE: des eaux turquoise (no -s)! |

| **indigo** | Indigo / Deep violet-blue | `a(n)-dee-GOH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=indigo&op=translate) | `Adjective (invariable)` | Le ciel crépusculaire prend une teinte indigo profonde. *(The twilight sky takes on a deep indigo hue.)* | Invariable | Nasal vowel in- [ɛ̃diɡo]. Derived from plant/dye, invariable: des reflets indigo. |
| **écarlate** | Scarlet / Bright vivid red | `ay-kar-LAHT` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=%C3%A9carlate&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Le cardinal est vêtu d'une toge écarlate. *(The cardinal is dressed in a scarlet robe.)* | écarlates (takes -s in plural) | Exception to rule: écarlate DOES take -s in plural: des capes écarlates [ekaʁlat]. |
| **pourpre** | Crimson / Purple-red | `POOR-pruh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=pourpre&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Le soleil couchant colore l'horizon d'un rouge pourpre. *(The setting sun colors the horizon in a crimson red.)* | pourpres (takes -s in plural) | False friend: in French, 'pourpre' is deep reddish-crimson, NOT regular violet/purple (which is violet). Takes -s in plural. |
| **fuchsia** | Fuchsia / Bright magenta pink | `fyook-SYAH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=fuchsia&op=translate) | `Adjective (invariable)` | Elle porte un chemisier fuchsia très éclatant. *(She is wearing a very radiant fuchsia blouse.)* | Invariable (never adds -s or -e) | Pronounced [fyksja] or [fyʃja]. Derived from flower name, strictly INVARIABLE: des robes fuchsia. |
| **kaki** | Khaki / Olive-drab | `kah-KEE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=kaki&op=translate) | `Adjective (invariable)` | Cette veste militaire kaki est en toile imperméable. *(This khaki military jacket is made of waterproof canvas.)* | Invariable (never adds -s or -e) | Derived from Persian via English, strictly INVARIABLE: des pantalons kaki (no -s) [kaki]! |
| **ocre** | Ochre / Earthy yellow-orange | `OH-kruh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=ocre&op=translate) | `Adjective (invariable)` | Les façades ocre du village de Roussillon illuminent la colline. *(The ochre facades of Roussillon illuminate the hill.)* | Invariable (never adds -s or -e) | Derived from natural mineral pigment, strictly INVARIABLE: des falaises ocre [ɔkʁ]. |
| **caramel** | Caramel-colored / Warm toffee brown | `kah-rah-MEL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=caramel&op=translate) | `Adjective (invariable)` | Elle porte des bottes en daim couleur caramel. *(She wears caramel-colored suede boots.)* | Invariable (never adds -s or -e) | Derived from food item, strictly INVARIABLE: des vestes caramel [kaʁamɛl]. |

Derived from food item, strictly INVARIABLE: des rideaux crème. |

As noun, plural is coraux; as color adjective, strictly INVARIABLE: des jupes corail. |

| **menthe** | Mint green | `MAHNT` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=menthe&op=translate) | `Adjective (invariable)` | Il a peint les volets dans un vert menthe très doux. *(He painted the shutters in a very soft mint green.)* | Invariable (never adds -s or -e) | Derived from herb, strictly INVARIABLE: des assiettes menthe [mɑ̃t]. |

Derived from chat (like cat's eyes at night). |

Opposite of éclatant. |

[dekɔlɔʁe]. Feminine adds silent '-e'. |
| **monochrome** | Monochrome / Single-colored | `moh-noh-KROHM` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=monochrome&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | L'artiste contemporain a créé une œuvre monochrome bleue. *(The contemporary artist created a blue monochrome work.)* | monochromes | Greek prefix mono- (single) + chrome (color) [mɔnɔkʁom]. |
| **fluo / fluorescent** | Fluorescent / Neon (high-vis) | `flyoo-OH / flyoo-oh-reh-SAHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=fluo%20/%20fluorescent&op=translate) | `Adjective (fluo invariable)` | Les cyclistes doivent porter un gilet jaune fluo la nuit. *(Cyclists must wear a high-vis neon yellow vest at night.)* | fluo is invariable: des vestes fluo | 'Fluo' is universally used in spoken French and is invariable: des baskets fluo [flyo]. |
| **harmoniser les couleurs** | To harmonize / match colors | `ar-moh-nee-ZAY lay koo-LUHR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=harmoniser%20les%20couleurs&op=translate) | `Expression / Verb harmoniser` | L'architecte d'intérieur sait harmoniser les couleurs avec élégance. *(The interior designer knows how to match colors with elegance.)* | j'harmonise, nous harmonisons | Silent 'h' [aʁmɔnize]. Single 's' pronounced [z]. |
| **éclaircir** | To lighten / To brighten (color) | `ay-klair-SEER` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=%C3%A9claircir&op=translate) | `Verb (2nd group -ir)` | Ajoutez une goutte de peinture blanche pour éclaircir la teinte. *(Add a drop of white paint to lighten the shade.)* | j'éclaircis, tu éclaircis, il éclaircit, nous éclaircissons, vous éclaircissez, ils éclaircissent | Regular 2nd group verb with -iss- infix: nous éclaircissons [eklɛʁsisɔ̃]. |

## 9. Nationalities and Countries (Les nationalités et les pays)

| French (with Article) | English Meaning | Pronunciation | Audio | Gender / Word Type | Example Sentence | Plural / Conjugation | Liaison & Teacher's Notes |
|:---|:---|:---|:---:|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| **l'Australie (f.)** | Australia | `lohs-trah-LEE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27Australie%20%28f.%29&op=translate) | `Feminine Country (f.)` | Canberra est la capitale de l'Australie. *(Canberra is the capital of Australia.)* | Preposition: en Australie | Feminine country. Preposition: en Australie [ɑ̃.n‿ostʁali] with [n] liaison. |
| **australien / australienne** | Australian (m./f.) | `ohs-trah-LYA(N) / ohs-trah-LYEN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=australien%20/%20australienne&op=translate) | `Nationality / Adjective (m./f.)` | Cette surfeuse australienne a remporté la compétition. *(This Australian surfer won the competition.)* | australiens / australiennes | Pattern -ien -> -ienne: doubles 'n' [ostʁaljɛ̃] / [ostʁaljɛn]. |
| **le Brésil** | Brazil | `luh bray-ZEEL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20Br%C3%A9sil&op=translate) | `Masculine Country (m.)` | Le carnaval de Rio attire des visiteurs au Brésil chaque année. *(The Rio carnival attracts visitors to Brazil every year.)* | Preposition: au Brésil (à + le = au) | Masculine country: au Brésil [o bʁezil]. From: du Brésil. |
| **brésilien / brésilienne** | Brazilian (m./f.) | `bray-zee-LYA(N) / bray-zee-LYEN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=br%C3%A9silien%20/%20br%C3%A9silienne&op=translate) | `Nationality / Adjective (m./f.)` | La musique brésilienne est entraînante et festive. *(Brazilian music is lively and festive.)* | brésiliens / brésiliennes | Pattern -ien -> -ienne: doubles 'n' [bʁeziljɛ̃] / [bʁeziljɛn]. |
| **le Mexique** | Mexico | `luh mek-ZEEK` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20Mexique&op=translate) | `Masculine Country (m. exception)` | Nous partons en voyage au Mexique cet hiver. *(We are going on a trip to Mexico this winter.)* | Preposition: au Mexique (à + le = au) | Gender breaker! Ends in '-e' but is MASCULINE (le Mexique, le Cambodge, le Mozambique, le Zimbabwe)! Preposition: au Mexique. |
| **mexicain / mexicaine** | Mexican (m./f.) | `mek-zee-KA(N) / mek-zee-KEN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=mexicain%20/%20mexicaine&op=translate) | `Nationality / Adjective (m./f.)` | La gastronomie mexicaine est réputée pour ses épices savoureuses. *(Mexican cuisine is renowned for its flavorful spices.)* | mexicains / mexicaines | Masculine nasal [mɛksikɛ̃]; feminine oral [mɛksikɛn]. |
| **le Maroc** | Morocco | `luh mah-ROHK` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20Maroc&op=translate) | `Masculine Country (m.)` | Marrakech est une ville touristique majeure au Maroc. *(Marrakech is a major tourist city in Morocco.)* | Preposition: au Maroc (à + le = au) | Masculine country. Preposition: au Maroc [o maʁɔk]. French is widely spoken in Morocco. |
| **marocain / marocaine** | Moroccan (m./f.) | `mah-roh-KA(N) / mah-roh-KEN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=marocain%20/%20marocaine&op=translate) | `Nationality / Adjective (m./f.)` | Le thé à la menthe marocain est servi très chaud avec des pâtisseries. *(Moroccan mint tea is served very hot with pastries.)* | marocains / marocaines | Masculine nasal [maʁɔkɛ̃]; feminine sounds 'n' [maʁɔkɛn]. |
| **l'Algérie (f.)** | Algeria | `lahl-zhay-REE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27Alg%C3%A9rie%20%28f.%29&op=translate) | `Feminine Country (f.)` | Alger est la capitale côtière de l'Algérie. *(Algiers is the coastal capital of Algeria.)* | Preposition: en Algérie | Feminine country. | **démissionner** | To resign / To quit one's job | `day-mee-syoh-NAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=d%C3%A9missionner&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group -er)` | Elle a décidé de démissionner pour lancer sa propre entreprise. *(She decided to resign to launch her own business.)* | je démissionne, tu démissionnes, il démissionne, nous démissionnons, vous démissionnez, ils démissionnent | Doubles 'ss' and 'nn' [demisjɔne]. Noun: la démission (resignation). |
| **être embauché / embauchée** | To be hired / recruited | `EH-truh ahn-boh-SHAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=%C3%AAtre%20embauch%C3%A9%20/%20embauch%C3%A9e&op=translate) | `Passive Verbal Locution` | Après trois entretiens concluants, elle a été embauchée ! *(After three successful interviews, she was hired!)* | embauchés / embauchées | Regular participle agreement. Noun: l'embauche (f.) [ɑ̃boʃ]. |
| **prendre sa retraite** | To retire (from work) | `PRAHN-druh sah ruh-TRET` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=prendre%20sa%20retraite&op=translate) | `Idiomatic Expression` | Mon père prend sa retraite à soixante-quatre ans. *(My father retires at 64 years old.)* | je prends ma retraite, nous prenons notre retraite | Irregular verb prendre. Retiree = un retraité / une retraitée. |
| **gagner sa vie** | To earn a living | `gah-NYAY sah VEE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=gagner%20sa%20vie&op=translate) | `Idiomatic Expression` | Il gagne bien sa vie grâce à son travail dans l'informatique. *(He earns a good living thanks to his work in IT.)* | je gagne ma vie, nous gagnons notre vie | Palatal gn [ɲ] [ɡaɲe]. Gagner sa vie = earn one's living. |
| **exercer un métier** | To practice a profession / To work as | `eg-zair-SAY œ̃ may-TYAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=exercer%20un%20m%C3%A9tier&op=translate) | `Expression / Verb exercer` | Elle exerce le métier de juge depuis plus de dix ans. *(She has practiced as a judge for more than 10 years.)* | j'exerce, nous exerçons (cedilla ç), vous exercez | Takes cedilla 'ç' before 'o' in nous exerçons [ɛɡzɛʁsɔ̃]. Formal idiom. |

Nasal vowel. Expression: avoir du front = to have nerve/audacity. |

Plural: [sil]. Mandatory [z] liaison: des‿cils no liaison, but les‿yeux. |

Lipstick = le rouge à lèvres. |

Feminine noun. |

'Cou' = entire neck; 'nuque' = back/nape. |

Do not confuse with 'le temple' (masculine religious temple). |

Masculine noun. |

Masculine plural. |

Foie gras = fattened duck/goose liver. |

Expression: 'avoir mal aux reins' in everyday French means lower backache! |
| **l'os (m.) / les os (m. pl.)** | Bone / Bones | `LOHSS / lay ZOH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27os%20%28m.%29%20/%20les%20os%20%28m.%20pl.%29&op=translate) | `Irregular Pronunciation Noun (m.)` | Le squelette humain compte deux cent six os au total. *(The human skeleton has 206 bones in total.)* | Pronunciation shift: un os [ɔs] -> les os [o] | Sound shift: final 's' IS sounded in singular: [ɔs]; silent in plural: [o] with [z] liaison: les‿os [le-z-o]! |

Expression: avoir du sang-froid = to be cool-headed. |

Cuisses de grenouille = frogs' legs. |

Doubles 'll'. |

Do not confuse with 'la pomme' (apple, double m) [pɔm]. |

## 12. Physical Descriptions of People (La description physique)

| French (with Article) | English Meaning | Pronunciation | Audio | Gender / Word Type | Example Sentence | Plural / Conjugation | Liaison & Teacher's Notes |
|:---|:---|:---|:---:|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| **de taille moyenne** | Of average height / medium height | `duh TAH-yuh mwah-YEN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=de%20taille%20moyenne&op=translate) | `Adjectival Phrase (invariable)` | Le suspect est un homme de taille moyenne avec un manteau sombre. *(The suspect is a man of average height with a dark coat.)* | Invariable descriptive phrase | Liquid -aille [taj] and -yenne [mwajɛn]. Very natural spoken description. |
| **svelte** | Slender / Slim / Willowy | `SVELT` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=svelte&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Cette danseuse étoile a une silhouette svelte et gracieuse. *(This prima ballerina has a slender and graceful figure.)* | sveltes | Epicene ending in -e: identical m./f. [svɛlt]. Elegant synonym of mince. |
| **corpulent / corpulente** | Stout / Heavy-set / Large build | `kor-pyoo-LAHN / kor-pyoo-LAHNT` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=corpulent%20/%20corpulente&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | C'est un homme corpulent à la voix grave et chaleureuse. *(He is a stout man with a deep and warm voice.)* | corpulents / corpulentes | Masculine silent 't' [kɔʁpylɑ̃]; feminine sounds 't' [kɔʁpylɑ̃t]. Polite term for heavy. |
| **trapu / trapue** | Stocky / Thickset / Squat | `trah-PYOO` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=trapu%20/%20trapue&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Ce joueur de rugby est petit mais particulièrement trapu et puissant. *(This rugby player is short but particularly stocky and powerful.)* | trapus / trapues | Pronounced identically m./f. [tʁapy]. Feminine adds silent '-e'. |
| **élancé / élancée** | Lanky / Tall and slender | `ay-lahn-SAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=%C3%A9lanc%C3%A9%20/%20%C3%A9lanc%C3%A9e&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Cet adolescent élancé a grandi de dix centimètres en un an. *(This lanky teenager grew ten centimeters in one year.)* | élancés / élancées | Cedilla ç ensures [s] sound before 'e' [elɑ̃se]. Feminine adds silent '-e'. |
| **athlétique** | Athletic / Toned / Well-built | `aht-lay-TEEK` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=athl%C3%A9tique&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Grâce à la natation quotidienne, il a une carrure très athlétique. *(Thanks to daily swimming, he has a very athletic build.)* | athlétiques | Epicene ending in -e [atletik]. Silent 'h'. |
| **les cheveux frisés** | Frizzy hair / Kinky curls | `lay shuh-VUH free-ZAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=les%20cheveux%20fris%C3%A9s&op=translate) | `Noun phrase (m. pl.)` | Elle porte fièrement ses magnifiques cheveux frisés au naturel. *(She proudly wears her magnificent frizzy hair naturally.)* | frisé agrees in m. pl.: frisés | Single 's' pronounced [z] [fʁize]. Tighter curls than bouclé. |
| **les cheveux ondulés** | Wavy hair | `lay shuh-VUH ohn-dyoo-LAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=les%20cheveux%20ondul%C3%A9s&op=translate) | `Noun phrase (m. pl.)` | Ses longs cheveux ondulés tombent en cascade sur ses épaules. *(Her long wavy hair cascades down her shoulders.)* | ondulé agrees in m. pl.: ondulés | Derived from onde (wave) [ɔ̃dyle]. Soft natural waves. |
| **les cheveux châtains** | Chestnut-brown hair / Brown hair | `lay shuh-VUH shah-TA(N)` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=les%20cheveux%20ch%C3%A2tains&op=translate) | `Noun phrase (m. pl.)` | Mon frère a les yeux noisette et les cheveux châtains. *(My brother has hazel eyes and chestnut-brown hair.)* | châtains (takes -s in plural) | Circumflex on 'â' [ʃatɛ̃]. In French, brown hair is called 'châtain', not brun (brun is very dark/black). |
| **les cheveux poivre et sel** | Salt-and-pepper hair (graying hair) | `lay shuh-VUH PWAH-vruh ay SEL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=les%20cheveux%20poivre%20et%20sel&op=translate) | `Idiomatic Phrase (invariable)` | À cinquante ans, il a de beaux cheveux poivre et sel bien soignés. *(At 50, he has handsome, well-groomed salt-and-pepper hair.)* | Invariable adjective phrase: des cheveux poivre et sel | Literally 'pepper and salt' [pwavʁ e sɛl]. Invariable phrase. |
| **la queue-de-cheval** | Ponytail (hairstyle) | `lah KUH duh shuh-VAHL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20queue-de-cheval&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Elle attache ses longs cheveux en queue-de-cheval pour faire du sport. *(She ties her long hair in a ponytail to do sports.)* | les queues-de-cheval | Literally 'horse's tail' [kø d‿ʃəval]. Plural: only queue takes -s. |
| **la tresse** | Braid / Plait | `lah TRESS` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20tresse&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | La petite fille porte deux tresses nouées avec des rubans roses. *(The little girl wears two braids tied with pink ribbons.)* | les tresses | Doubles 'ss' [tʁɛs]. Verb: tresser (to braid). |
| **la frange** | Bangs / Fringe | `lah FRAHNZH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20frange&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Elle a coupé sa frange droite juste au-dessus des sourcils. *(She cut her straight bangs right above her eyebrows.)* | les franges | Feminine noun [fʁɑ̃ʒ]. Singular in French (la frange), unlike English plural 'bangs'. |
| **le chignon** | Hair bun / Updo | `luh shee-NYOHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20chignon&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | La ballerine a les cheveux tirés en un chignon strict. *(The ballerina has her hair pulled back into a neat bun.)* | les chignons | Palatal gn [ɲ] [ʃiɲɔ̃]. Masculine noun. |
| **le teint** | Complexion / Skin tone | `luh TA(N)` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20teint&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Boire de l'eau donne un teint frais et lumineux. *(Drinking water gives a fresh and luminous complexion.)* | les teints | Silent 't' in nasal [tɛ̃]. Fond de teint = liquid makeup foundation. |
| **le teint mat** | Olive complexion / Tan skin tone | `luh TA(N) MAHT` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20teint%20mat&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Les habitants du bassin méditerranéen ont souvent le teint mat. *(Inhabitants of the Mediterranean basin often have olive complexions.)* | les teints mats | Sounded 't' in mat [mat]. |
| **le teint clair** | Fair complexion / Pale skin tone | `luh TA(N) KLAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20teint%20clair&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Avec son teint clair, elle doit mettre de la crème solaire indice 50. *(With her fair complexion, she must wear SPF 50 sunscreen.)* | les teints clairs | Sounded 'r' in clair [klɛʁ]. |
| **bronzé / bronzée** | Sun-tanned / Tanned | `brohn-ZAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=bronz%C3%A9%20/%20bronz%C3%A9e&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Ils rentrent de deux semaines à la mer tout bronzés. *(They return from two weeks at the sea completely tanned.)* | bronzés / bronzées | Derived from bronze [bʁɔ̃ze]. Verb: bronzer (to sunbathe/tan). |
| **une cicatrice** | A scar | `oon see-kah-TREESS` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=une%20cicatrice&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Il a une petite cicatrice au-dessus du sourcil gauche depuis l'enfance. *(He has a small scar above his left eyebrow since childhood.)* | les cicatrices | Feminine noun [sikkatʁis]. Verb: cicatriser (to heal/scar over). |
| **un grain de beauté** | A beauty mark / A mole | `œ̃ GRA(N) duh boh-TAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=un%20grain%20de%20beaut%C3%A9&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Elle a un joli grain de beauté sur la joue gauche. *(She has a pretty beauty mark on her left cheek.)* | des grains de beauté | Charming French expression literally meaning 'seed of beauty'! |
| **une fossette** | A dimple (on cheek or chin) | `oon foh-SET` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=une%20fossette&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Son visage s'illumine de deux jolies fossettes quand elle sourit. *(Her face lights up with two cute dimples when she smiles.)* | les fossettes | Doubles 'ss' and 'tt' in fossette [fɔsɛt]. Feminine noun. |
| **les rides (f. pl.)** | Wrinkles (facial lines) | `lay REED` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=les%20rides%20%28f.%20pl.%29&op=translate) | `Feminine Plural (f. pl.)` | Ce vieux marin a le visage buriné et marqué par de profondes rides. *(This old sailor has a weathered face marked by deep wrinkles.)* | une ride -> les rides | Feminine noun [ʁid]. Anti-rides = anti-wrinkle cream. |
| **avoir de l'allure** | To have style / poise / great presence | `ah-VWAHR duh lah-LYOOR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=avoir%20de%20l%27allure&op=translate) | `Idiomatic Description Locution` | Même habillé simplement, cet homme a beaucoup d'allure. *(Even dressed simply, this man has great style and presence.)* | il a de l'allure, elle a de l'allure | Liaison: d'allure [dalyʁ]. High praise for innate elegance and carriage. |
| **soigné / soignée** | Well-groomed / Neat / Impeccable | `swah-NYAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=soign%C3%A9%20/%20soign%C3%A9e&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Il se présente toujours avec une coiffure et une tenue très soignées. *(He always presents himself with very neat hair and attire.)* | soignés / soignées | Palatal gn [ɲ] [swaɲe]. Feminine adds silent '-e'. |
| **négligé / négligée** | Disheveled / Sloppy / Untidy | `nay-glee-ZHAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=n%C3%A9glig%C3%A9%20/%20n%C3%A9glig%C3%A9e&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Il a l'air un peu négligé avec sa chemise froissée. *(He looks a bit disheveled with his wrinkled shirt.)* | négligés / négligées | Pronounced identically m./f. [neɡliʒe]. Opposite of soigné. |
| **charismatique** | Charismatic / Compelling | `kah-rees-mah-TEEK` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=charismatique&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | L'orateur charismatique captive l'auditoire dès les premiers mots. *(The charismatic speaker captivates the audience from the very first words.)* | charismatiques | 'ch' pronounced [k]: [kaʁismatik]! Epicene ending in -e. |
| **séduisant / séduisante** | Attractive / Charming / Alluring | `say-dwee-ZAHN / say-dwee-ZAHNT` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=s%C3%A9duisant%20/%20s%C3%A9duisante&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Elle a une personnalité chaleureuse et un sourire très séduisant. *(She has a warm personality and a very attractive smile.)* | séduisants / séduisantes | Masculine silent 't' [sedɥizɑ̃]; feminine sounds 't' [sedɥizɑ̃t]. |
| **grossir** | To gain weight / To get bigger | `groh-SEER` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=grossir&op=translate) | `Verb (2nd group -ir)` | Il a un peu grossi pendant les fêtes de fin d'année. *(He gained a bit of weight during the end-of-year holidays.)* | je grossis, tu grossis, il grossit, nous grossissons, vous grossissez, ils grossissent | 2nd group regular verb with -iss- infix: nous grossissons [ɡʁosisɔ̃]. Opposite: maigrir. |
| **maigrir** | To lose weight / To slim down | `meh-GREER` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=maigrir&op=translate) | `Verb (2nd group -ir)` | Elle a maigri de trois kilos en pratiquant la course à pied. *(She lost three kilos by taking up running.)* | je maigris, tu maigris, il maigrit, nous maigrissons, vous maigrissez, ils maigrissent | 2nd group regular verb with -iss- infix: nous maigrissons [mɛɡʁisɔ̃]. Opposite: grossir. |
| **vieillir** | To age / To grow older | `vyay-YEER` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=vieillir&op=translate) | `Verb (2nd group -ir)` | Il vieillit avec grâce et garde toute son énergie vitale. *(He ages gracefully and keeps all his vital energy.)* | je vieillis, tu vieillis, il vieillit, nous vieillissons, vous vieillissez, ils vieillissent | 2nd group regular verb: nous vieillissons [vjɛjisɔ̃]. Liquid -ill- [vjɛjiʁ]. |

| French (with Article) | English Meaning | Pronunciation | Audio | Gender / Word Type | Example Sentence | Plural / Conjugation | Liaison & Teacher's Notes |

| **le chemisier** | Woman's blouse | `luh shuh-mee-ZYAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20chemisier&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Elle porte un chemisier en soie blanche très chic. *(She wears a very chic white silk blouse.)* | les chemisiers | Masculine noun [ʃəmizje]. False friend: French robe = dress; peignoir = bathrobe! |

Very popular winter wear throughout France. |

Distinction: 'écharpe' = heavy winter wool scarf; 'foulard' = silk/light fashion scarf. |

Feminine in French: des tongs. |

Masculine noun. |

Dual meaning: jewelry brooch and roasting spit. |

Compound feminine noun. |

| **rénover** | To renovate / To refurbish | `ray-noh-VAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=r%C3%A9nover&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group -er)` | Les nouveaux propriétaires vont entièrement rénover la cuisine et la salle d'eau. *(The new owners are going to fully renovate the kitchen and shower room.)* | je rénove, tu rénoves, il rénove, nous rénovons, vous rénovez, ils rénovent | Regular -er verb [renɔve]. Noun: la rénovation. |
| **s'installer** | To settle in / To move into a home | `sa(n)-stah-LAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=s%27installer&op=translate) | `Reflexive Verb (1st group -er)` | Nous venons de nous installer dans notre nouvel appartement bordelais. *(We have just settled into our new Bordeaux apartment.)* | je m'installe, tu t'installes, il s'installe, nous nous installons, vous vous installez, ils s'installent | Reflexive verb [sɛ̃stale]. Doubles 'll'. |

| **la bouilloire** | Electric kettle / Kettle | `lah bwee-YWAHR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20bouilloire&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Fais bouillir de l'eau fraîche dans la bouilloire électrique pour infuser le thé. *(Boil fresh water in the electric kettle to brew tea.)* | les bouilloires | Liquid -ouill- [bujwaʁ]. Feminine ending in -oire. |
| **la cafetière** | Coffee maker / Coffee pot | `lah kah-fuh-TYAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20cafeti%C3%A8re&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Allume la cafetière filtre dès ton réveil pour avoir du café chaud. *(Turn on the drip coffee maker as soon as you wake up to have hot coffee.)* | les cafetières | Feminine noun [kaftjɛʁ]. Grave accent on 'è'. |
| **le mixeur / le blender** | Blender / Hand mixer | `luh meek-SUHR / luh BLEN-duhr` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20mixeur%20/%20le%20blender&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Utilise le mixeur plongeant pour mixer la soupe de potiron veloutée. *(Use the immersion hand blender to puree the velvety pumpkin soup.)* | les mixeurs | Masculine noun [miksœʁ]. Mixeur plongeant = immersion hand blender. |
| **le fer à lisser** | Hair straightener / Flat iron | `luh FAIR ah lee-SAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20fer%20%C3%A0%20lisser&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Elle utilise son fer à lisser en céramique pour coiffer ses cheveux rebelles. *(She uses her ceramic flat iron to style her unruly hair.)* | les fers à lisser | Lisser = to smooth/straighten [fɛʁ a lise]. |
| **le sèche-cheveux** | Hair dryer / Blow dryer | `luh SESH-shuh-VUH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20s%C3%A8che-cheveux&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Sèche tes cheveux mouillés avec le sèche-cheveux avant de sortir dans le froid. *(Dry your wet hair with the hair dryer before going out in the cold.)* | les sèche-cheveux (invariable) | Grave accent on 'è' [sɛʃʃəvø]. Cheveux keeps plural 'x'. |
| **le ventilateur** | Fan / Electric cooling fan | `luh vahn-tee-lah-TUHR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20ventilateur&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Mets en marche le ventilateur pour créer une brise rafraîchissante dans le salon. *(Turn on the electric fan to create a refreshing breeze in the living room.)* | les ventilateurs | Masculine noun suffix -teur [vɑ̃tilatœʁ]. |
| **l'armoire à pharmacie (f.)** | Medicine cabinet | `lar-MWAHR ah far-mah-SEE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27armoire%20%C3%A0%20pharmacie%20%28f.%29&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Range les pansements et désinfectants en hauteur dans l'armoire à pharmacie. *(Store bandages and disinfectants up high in the medicine cabinet.)* | les armoires à pharmacie | Elision with l' [aʁmwaʁ a faʁmasi]. Must be locked out of children's reach. |
| **débrancher** | To unplug / To disconnect | `day-brahn-SHAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=d%C3%A9brancher&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group -er)` | Débranchez toujours les appareils électriques avant de partir en vacances prolongées. *(Always unplug electric appliances before going on extended vacations.)* | je débranche, tu débranches, il débranche, nous débranchons, vous débranchez, ils débranchent | Prefix dé- denotes reversal [debʁɑ̃ʃe]. Opposite of brancher. |
| **plier** | To fold (clothes, bedding, furniture) | `plee-AY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=plier&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group -er)` | Aide-moi à plier ce grand drap propre avant de le ranger dans l'armoire. *(Help me fold this large clean bedsheet before storing it in the wardrobe.)* | je plie, tu plies, il plie, nous plions, vous pliez, ils plient | Regular -er verb [plije]. Table pliante = folding table. |
| **empiler** | To stack / To pile up | `ahn-pee-LAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=empiler&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group -er)` | Nous empilons les chaises en plastique pour libérer de l'espace dans la cour. *(We stack the plastic chairs to free up space in the courtyard.)* | j'empile, tu empiles, il empile, nous empilons, vous empilez, ils empilent | Regular -er verb [ɑ̃pile]. Chaises empilables = stackable chairs. |

|:---|:---|:---|:---:|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| **émerger du sommeil** | To surface from sleep / To gradually wake up | `ay-mair-ZHAY dyoo soh-MEH-yuh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=%C3%A9merger%20du%20sommeil&op=translate) | `Verbal Expression` | Il me faut au moins vingt minutes pour émerger du sommeil chaque matin. *(It takes me at least twenty minutes to emerge from sleep each morning.)* | j'émerge, tu émerges, il émerge, nous émergeons, vous émergez, ils émergent | Spelling quirk: retains 'e' before -ons: nous émergeons [emeʁʒe]. Liquid -eil. |
| **sauter hors du lit** | To jump out of bed | `soh-TAY OR dyoo LEE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=sauter%20hors%20du%20lit&op=translate) | `Verbal Expression` | Dès que l'alarme sonne, elle saute hors du lit pleine d'énergie. *(As soon as the alarm rings, she jumps out of bed full of energy.)* | je saute, tu sautes, il saute, nous sautons, vous sautez, ils sautent | Aspirated 'h' in hors (h aspiré): NO liaison: saute hors [sot ɔʁ]. |
| **éteindre le réveil** | To turn off the alarm / To switch off clock | `ay-TA(N)-druh luh ray-VEH-yuh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=%C3%A9teindre%20le%20r%C3%A9veil&op=translate) | `Verbal Expression` | J'éteins machinalement le réveil pour gagner quelques minutes de sommeil. *(I mechanically turn off the alarm clock to gain a few minutes of sleep.)* | j'éteins, tu éteins, il éteint, nous éteignons, vous éteignez, ils éteignent | Irregular 3rd group verb [etɛ̃dʁ]. Stem changes to éteign- in plural. |

Noun: la somnolence. |
| **s'étirer** | To stretch (one's body/muscles) | `say-tee-RAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=s%27%C3%A9tirer&op=translate) | `Reflexive Verb (1st group -er)` | Je m'étire longuement les bras et le dos en sortant des couvertures. *(I stretch my arms and back for a long time getting out of the covers.)* | je m'étire, tu t'étires, il s'étire, nous nous étirons, vous vous étirez, ils s'étirent | Reflexive verb [setikʁe]. Noun: l'étirement (m.). |
| **bâiller** | To yawn | `bah-YAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=b%C3%A2iller&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group -er)` | Elle ne peut s'empêcher de bâiller à plusieurs reprises pendant la réunion matinale. *(She cannot help yawning several times during the morning meeting.)* | je bâille, tu bâilles, il bâille, nous bâillons, vous bâillez, ils bâillent | Circumflex on 'â' [bɑje]. Liquid -ïll-. |
| **se frotter les yeux** | To rub one's eyes | `suh froh-TAY lay ZYUH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=se%20frotter%20les%20yeux&op=translate) | `Verbal Expression` | L'enfant se frotte les yeux avec ses petites mains pour chasser la fatigue. *(The child rubs his eyes with his little hands to chase away tiredness.)* | je me frotte les yeux, tu te frottes les yeux, il se frotte les yeux, nous nous frottons les yeux, vous vous frottez les yeux, ils se frottent les yeux | Doubles 'tt' in frotter [fʁɔte]. Liaison: les‿yeux [le zjø]. |

Nasal -ain [bɛ̃]. |
| **se savonner** | To soap oneself / To lather up | `suh sah-voh-NAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=se%20savonner&op=translate) | `Reflexive Verb (1st group -er)` | Il se savonne soigneusement sous le jet d'eau tiède avec du savon de Marseille. *(He soaps himself thoroughly under the warm stream of water with Marseille soap.)* | je me savonne, tu te savonnes, il se savonne, nous nous savonnons, vous vous savonnez, ils se savonnent | Doubles 'nn' [savɔne]. Derived from 'le savon'. |

Note: in French use 'les cheveux', not possessive. |

Noun: le maquillage. |

Disque démaquillant = cotton pad. |

Dual meaning: clothing belt and car/airplane safety belt. |

Imperative: Dépêche-toi ! |

Also 'badger'. |

'Micro-sieste' is popular in modern office wellness. |
| **faire une coupure** | To take a midday break / Split shift break | `FAIR yoon koo-PYOOR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=faire%20une%20coupure&op=translate) | `Verbal Expression` | Les restaurateurs font souvent une longue coupure entre le service de midi et du soir. *(Restaurateurs often take a long break between the midday and evening services.)* | je fais une coupure, nous faisons une coupure | In French labor law, 'horaires coupés' refers to split shifts [kupyʁ]. |
| **souffler un peu** | To catch one's breath / To take a breather | `soo-FLAY a(n) PUH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=souffler%20un%20peu&op=translate) | `Verbal Expression` | Après une dure journée de travail, je m'assois dans le canapé pour souffler un peu. *(After a hard day at work, I sit down on the sofa to catch my breath a bit.)* | je souffle, tu souffles, il souffle, nous soufflons, vous soufflez, ils soufflent | Doubles 'ff' [sufle]. Colloquial and natural. |

Common security ritual. |
| **fermer les volets** | To close the window shutters (night routine) | `fair-MAY lay voh-LEH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=fermer%20les%20volets&op=translate) | `Verbal Expression` | À la tombée de la nuit, nous faisons le tour de la maison pour fermer les volets. *(At nightfall, we make the rounds of the house to close the shutters.)* | je ferme les volets, tu fermes, il ferme, nous fermons, vous fermez, ils ferment | Silent 't' in volet [vɔlɛ]. Traditional evening ritual in French homes. |
| **baisser la lumière** | To dim the light / Turn down lighting | `beh-SAY lah lyoo-MYAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=baisser%20la%20lumi%C3%A8re&op=translate) | `Verbal Expression` | Baisse la lumière pour créer une atmosphère propice au repos et au sommeil. *(Dim the light to create an atmosphere conducive to rest and sleep.)* | je baisse, tu baisses, il baisse, nous baissons, vous baissez, ils baissent | Doubles 'ss' [bɛse]. Opposite: monter / augmenter la lumière. |
| **ronfler** | To snore | `rohn-FLAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=ronfler&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group -er)` | Mon conjoint est tellement fatigué ce soir qu'il se met à ronfler profondément. *(My partner is so tired tonight that he starts snoring deeply.)* | je ronfle, tu ronfles, il ronfle, nous ronflons, vous ronflez, ils ronflent | Nasal on- [ʁɔ̃fle]. Noun: le ronflement. |

## 19. Common Household Chores (Les tâches ménagères)

| French (with Article) | English Meaning | Pronunciation | Audio | Gender / Word Type | Example Sentence | Plural / Conjugation | Liaison & Teacher's Notes |
|:---|:---|:---|:---:|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| **faire le grand ménage** | To do a deep cleaning / Spring cleaning | `FAIR luh GRAHN may-NAHZH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=faire%20le%20grand%20m%C3%A9nage&op=translate) | `Verbal Expression` | Ce samedi, nous faisons le grand ménage de fond en comble dans toute la maison. *(This Saturday, we are doing a deep clean from top to bottom throughout the house.)* | je fais le grand ménage, tu fais, il fait, nous faisons, vous faites, ils font | Liaison: le grand‿ménage [ɡʁɑ̃ menaʒ]. Nasal -an-. |
| **l'éponge (f.)** | Sponge / Cleaning sponge | `lay-POHNZH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27%C3%A9ponge%20%28f.%29&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Humidifie l'éponge avec de l'eau tiède avant d'essuyer la table de cuisine. *(Dampen the sponge with warm water before wiping the kitchen table.)* | les éponges | Feminine noun with elision l' [epɔ̃ʒ]. Idiom: 'passer l'éponge' = to wipe slate clean. |
| **le liquide vaisselle** | Dish soap / Dishwashing liquid | `luh lee-KEED veh-SEL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20liquide%20vaisselle&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Verse quelques gouttes de liquide vaisselle dégraissant sur l'éponge. *(Pour a few drops of grease-cutting dish soap onto the sponge.)* | les liquides vaisselle | Masculine noun [likid vɛsɛl]. | **le set de table** | Placemat | `luh SET duh TAH-bluh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20set%20de%20table&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Elle dispose un joli set de table tressé devant chaque convive. *(She lays out a pretty woven placemat in front of each guest.)* | les sets de table | Sounded 't' [sɛt də tabl]. Alternative to a full tablecloth (la nappe). |
| **la salière et la poivrière** | Salt shaker and pepper mill / set | `lah sah-LYAIR ay lah pwah-vree-YAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20sali%C3%A8re%20et%20la%20poivri%C3%A8re&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Passez-moi la salière et la poivrière situées au centre de la table, s'il vous plaît. *(Pass me the salt shaker and pepper mill in the center of the table, please.)* | les salières et poivrières | Feminine nouns ending in -ière [saljɛʁ e pwavʁijɛʁ]. |
| **la corbeille à pain** | Bread basket | `lah kor-BEH-yuh ah PA(N)` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20corbeille%20%C3%A0%20pain&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | La corbeille à pain en osier est garnie de tranches de baguette fraîche. *(The wicker bread basket is lined with slices of fresh baguette.)* | les corbeilles à pain | Liquid -eille- [kɔʁbɛj a pɛ̃]. Nasal -ain. |
| **tartiner** | To spread (butter, jam, spread) | `tar-tee-NAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=tartiner&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group -er)` | J'adore tartiner généreusement du beurre demi-sel et de la confiture sur mon pain grillé. *(I love generously spreading semi-salted butter and jam on my toast.)* | je tartine, tu tartines, il tartine, nous tartinons, vous tartinez, ils tartinent | Regular -er verb [taʁtine]. Noun: une tartine. |
| **saupoudrer** | To sprinkle / Dust lightly (sugar, cheese, salt) | `soh-poo-DRAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=saupoudrer&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group -er)` | Saupoudrez un peu de parmesan râpé sur votre assiette de pâtes fraîches. *(Dust some grated parmesan cheese over your plate of fresh pasta.)* | je saupoudre, tu saupoudres, il saupoudre, nous saupoudrons, vous saupoudrez, ils saupoudrent | Regular -er verb [sopudʁe]. From soupoudrer (sauce + poudre). |
| **assaisonner** | To season / To dress (salad, sauce, meat) | `ah-seh-zoh-NAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=assaisonner&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group -er)` | Assaisonnez la vinaigrette avec une pointe de moutarde de Dijon et de l'estragon. *(Season the vinaigrette dressing with a dash of Dijon mustard and tarragon.)* | j'assaisonne, tu assaisonnes, il assaisonne, nous assaisonnons, vous assaisonnez, ils assaisonnent | Doubles 'ss' and 'nn' [asɛzɔne]. Noun: l'assaisonnement. |
| **couper en rondelles** | To slice into rounds / rings (cucumber, carrots) | `koo-PAY ahn rohn-DEL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=couper%20en%20rondelles&op=translate) | `Verbal Expression` | Coupe les courgettes et concombres en fines rondelles pour la salade estivale. *(Slice the zucchinis and cucumbers into thin rounds for the summer salad.)* | je coupe, tu coupes, il coupe, nous coupons, vous coupez, ils coupent | Rondelle doubles 'll' [kupedɑ̃ ʁɔ̃dɛl]. Nasal -on-. |
| **couper en dés** | To dice / Cut into cubes (cheese, vegetables) | `koo-PAY ahn DAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=couper%20en%20d%C3%A9s&op=translate) | `Verbal Expression` | Le cuisinier coupe le fromage et les tomates fraîches en petits dés réguliers. *(The chef cuts the cheese and fresh tomatoes into small even cubes.)* | je coupe en dés, nous coupons en dés | Dés = dice/cubes [kupe ɑ̃ de]. In culinary French: couper en brunoise. |
| **reprendre une portion** | To have seconds / To take another helping | `ruh-PRAHN-druh yoon por-SYOHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=reprendre%20une%20portion&op=translate) | `Verbal Expression` | Le plat est tellement exquis que chaque invité reprend une deuxième portion. *(The dish is so exquisite that every guest has a second helping.)* | je reprends, tu reprends, il reprend, nous reprenons, vous reprenez, ils reprennent | Irregular verb reprendre [ʁəpʁɑ̃dʁ]. Noun: la portion. |
| **refuser poliment** | To decline politely (food, drink) | `ruh-fyoo-ZAY poh-lee-MAHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=refuser%20poliment&op=translate) | `Verbal Expression` | Rassasié après le repas copieux, il refuse poliment une seconde part de gâteau. *(Satisfied after the hearty meal, he politely declines a second slice of cake.)* | je refuse, tu refuses, il refuse, nous refusons, vous refusez, ils refusent | Regular -er verb [ʁəfyze pɔlimɑ̃]. Silent 't' in poliment. |
| **porter un toast** | To make a toast / Propose a toast | `por-TAY a(n) TOHST` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=porter%20un%20toast&op=translate) | `Verbal Expression` | Le marié se lève pour porter un toast émouvant à l'assemblée des invités. *(The groom stands up to propose a moving toast to the assembly of guests.)* | je porte un toast, tu portes, il porte, nous portons, vous portez, ils portent | Sounded 'st' [tɔst]. Alternative expression: lever son verre. |
| **être gourmand / gourmande** | To be a foodie / To love good food / Sweet tooth | `EH-truh goor-MAHN / goor-MAHND` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=%C3%AAtre%20gourmand%20/%20gourmande&op=translate) | `Adjectival Expression` | Très gourmande, elle ne peut jamais quitter la table sans savourer une douceur sucrée. *(A true foodie with a sweet tooth, she can never leave the table without savoring a sweet treat.)* | gourmands / gourmandes | In French culture, 'gourmand' is a compliment praising love of gastronomy [ɡuʁmɑ̃] / [ɡuʁmɑ̃d]. |

| French (with Article) | English Meaning | Pronunciation | Audio | Gender / Word Type | Example Sentence | Plural / Conjugation | Liaison & Teacher's Notes |
|:---|:---|:---|:---:|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| **un expresso** | Espresso / Short black coffee | `a(n) nek-spres-OH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=un%20expresso&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Le matin au comptoir du café, je commande un expresso bien serré. *(In the morning at the café counter, I order a strong, short espresso.)* | des expressos | Liaison: un‿expresso [œ̃n‿ɛkspʁɛso]. Also spelled espresso. |
| **un décaféiné / un déca** | Decaf coffee / Decaffeinated | `a(n) day-kah-fay-ee-NAY / a(n) day-KAH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=un%20d%C3%A9caf%C3%A9in%C3%A9%20/%20un%20d%C3%A9ca&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Le soir après le dîner, je préfère boire un déca pour ne pas troubler mon sommeil. *(In the evening after dinner, I prefer drinking a decaf so as not to disturb my sleep.)* | des décaféinés / des décas | Colloquially shortened to 'un déca' in all French cafés [deka]. |
| **un cappuccino** | Cappuccino (espresso with frothed milk) | `a(n) kah-poo-chee-NOH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=un%20cappuccino&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Le serveur saupoudre un peu de cacao amer sur la mousse de ce cappuccino. *(The waiter sprinkles some bitter cocoa on the foam of this cappuccino.)* | des cappuccinos | Italian loanword [kaputʃino]. Doubles 'pp' and 'cc'. |
| **un café noisette** | Espresso with a dash of milk (hazelnut-colored) | `a(n) kah-FAY nwah-ZET` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=un%20caf%C3%A9%20noisette&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Garçon, un café noisette au comptoir, s'il vous plaît ! *(Waiter, a hazelnut-colored coffee with a dash of milk at the counter, please!)* | des cafés noisette | Named for its hazel color, contains NO nuts, just a dash of milk/cream [kafe nwazɛt]! |
| **un thé vert** | Green tea | `a(n) TAY VAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=un%20th%C3%A9%20vert&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Ce thé vert parfumé au jasmin est très riche en antioxydants naturels. *(This jasmine-scented green tea is very rich in natural antioxidants.)* | des thés verts | Silent 't' in vert [te vɛʁ]. Silent 'h' in thé. |
| **un thé à la menthe** | Fresh mint tea | `a(n) TAY ah lah MAHN-tuh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=un%20th%C3%A9%20%C3%A0%20la%20menthe&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | On sert un thé à la menthe fumant et très sucré dans de jolis verres décorés. *(Piping hot and very sweet mint tea is served in pretty decorated glasses.)* | des thés à la menthe | Traditional Maghrebi tea widely cherished in France [te a la mɑ̃t]. |
| **la menthe à l'eau** | Mint cordial / Mint syrup mixed with still water | `lah MAHN-tah-LOH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20menthe%20%C3%A0%20l%27eau&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Les enfants commandent une menthe à l'eau bien fraîche avec des glaçons en terrasse. *(The children order a very chilled mint syrup with water and ice cubes on the terrace.)* | des menthes à l'eau | Green syrup diluted in tap water: classic French café drink for children [mɑ̃t a l‿o]. |
| **le diabolo menthe** | Lemonade with mint syrup | `luh dyah-boh-LOH MAHN-tuh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20diabolo%20menthe&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Un diabolo menthe ou fraise est un mélange pétillant de sirop et de limonade. *(A mint or strawberry diabolo is a fizzy mix of syrup and French lemonade.)* | les diabolos menthe | Iconic French café soda drink [djabɔlo mɑ̃t]. |
| **le panaché** | Shandy (half beer, half lemonade) | `luh pah-nah-SHAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20panach%C3%A9&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Ce panaché très frais est idéal pour se désaltérer sans trop d'alcool. *(This very chilled shandy is ideal for quenching thirst without too much alcohol.)* | les panachés | Low-alcohol summer mix [panaʃe]. Also sold pre-bottled in supermarkets. |

| **le kir** | Kir (aperitif of white wine with blackcurrant liqueur) | `luh KEER` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20kir&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Nous prenons un kir au cassis en apéritif avant le repas de fête. *(We have a blackcurrant Kir as an aperitif before the celebratory meal.)* | les kirs | Named after Canon Félix Kir, former mayor of Dijon [kiʁ]. Traditional Burgundy apéritif. |
| **le kir royal** | Kir Royal (champagne with blackcurrant liqueur) | `luh KEER rwah-YAHL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20kir%20royal&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Pour le réveillon, nous trinquons avec une coupe de kir royal pétillant. *(For New Year's Eve, we toast with a glass of sparkling Kir Royal.)* | les kirs royaux (irregular plural) | Plural of royal is royaux [kiʁ ʁwajal]. Made with champagne instead of still white wine. |
| **le pastis** | Pastis (anise-flavored spirit / Ricard) | `luh pahs-TEESS` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20pastis&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Dans le Midi de la France, le pastis se dilue avec cinq volumes d'eau glacée. *(In the South of France, pastis is diluted with five volumes of chilled water.)* | les pastis (invariable) | Final 's' is sounded [pastis]. Invariable plural. Turns milky white when water is added. |
| **le jus de tomate** | Tomato juice | `luh ZHOO duh toh-MAHT` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20jus%20de%20tomate&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Le serveur apporte un jus de tomate assaisonné avec du sel de céleri et du Tabasco. *(The waiter brings a tomato juice seasoned with celery salt and Tabasco.)* | les jus de tomate | Classic savory non-alcoholic café order in France [ʒy də tɔmat]. |
| **le jus de raisin** | Grape juice | `luh ZHOO duh reh-ZA(N)` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20jus%20de%20raisin&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Ce pur jus de raisin artisanal sans sucres ajoutés a un goût fruité intense. *(This artisanal pure grape juice with no added sugar has an intense fruity taste.)* | les jus de raisin | Nasal -in [ʁɛzɛ̃]. Raisin = grape (fresh), not raisin (which is 'raisin sec')! |

| **le milkshake** | Milkshake | `luh MEELK-shehk` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20milkshake&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Les adolescents commandent un grand milkshake onctueux au chocolat et à la vanille. *(The teenagers order a large creamy chocolate and vanilla milkshake.)* | les milkshakes | English borrowing [milkʃɛk]. Masculine noun. |

| **l'eau minérale (f.)** | Mineral water (bottled spring water) | `LOH mee-nay-RAHL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27eau%20min%C3%A9rale%20%28f.%29&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | La France est célèbre pour ses nombreuses sources d'eau minérale naturelle comme Évian ou Volvic. *(France is famous for its many natural mineral water springs such as Evian or Volvic.)* | les eaux minérales (irregular plural) | Plural of eau is eaux (-x), plural of minérale is minérales (-s) [o mineʁal]. |
| **l'eau du robinet (f.)** | Tap water | `LOH dyoo roh-bee-NEH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27eau%20du%20robinet%20%28f.%29&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | En France, l'eau du robinet est parfaitement potable et soumise à des contrôles stricts. *(In France, tap water is perfectly drinkable and subject to strict checks.)* | les eaux du robinet | Silent 't' in robinet [o dy ʁɔbinɛ]. Always free at French restaurants upon request. |
| **une carafe d'eau fraîche** | A pitcher of chilled water | `yoon kah-RAHF DOH FRESH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=une%20carafe%20d%27eau%20fra%C3%AEche&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Le serveur nous apporte d'office une carafe d'eau fraîche avec les verres. *(The waiter brings us a pitcher of chilled water automatically with the glasses.)* | des carafes d'eau fraîche | Feminine adjective fraîche with circumflex on 'î' [kaʁaf do fʁɛʃ]. |
| **une chope de bière** | A beer mug / Beer stein | `yoon SHOHP duh BYAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=une%20chope%20de%20bi%C3%A8re&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Il lève sa lourde chope de bière en verre épais pour célébrer la victoire. *(He raises his heavy thick-glass beer mug to celebrate the victory.)* | des chopes de bière | Feminine noun [ʃɔp də bjɛʁ]. |
| **un demi de bière** | A draft beer (25 cl glass) | `a(n) duh-MEE duh BYAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=un%20demi%20de%20bi%C3%A8re&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Garçon, remettez-nous un demi de bière blonde bien fraîche à la pression ! *(Waiter, bring us another chilled draft blonde beer!)* | des demis de bière | Standard French bar unit: 'un demi' equals 25 centiliters (quarter-liter, despite the name demi!) [dəmi]. |
| **une pinte de bière** | A pint of beer (50 cl glass) | `yoon PA(N)NT duh BYAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=une%20pinte%20de%20bi%C3%A8re&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Pendant l'happy hour, la pinte de 50 cl est au prix du demi. *(During happy hour, the 50 cl pint is at the price of a small draft.)* | des pintes de bière | Nasal in- [pɛ̃t]. In France, a pint is 50 cl (half a liter). |
| **la tireuse à bière** | Beer tap / Draft beer dispenser pump | `lah tee-RUHZ ah BYAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20tireuse%20%C3%A0%20bi%C3%A8re&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Le barman actionne la poignée de la tireuse à bière pour servir un demi mousseux. *(The bartender pulls the beer tap handle to serve a foamy draft beer.)* | les tireuses à bière | Feminine noun [tiʁøz a bjɛʁ]. |

Essential French kitchen and picnic tool. |

Used for crown caps (capsules). |

Also means to deposit money (verser de l'argent). |

Evokes relaxed French café culture. |
